Experiments on HumanAct12 and the refined NTU-RGBD (NTU-VIBE) dataset show that our approach consistently improves the accuracy of multiple skeleton-based action recognition models, validating its effectiveness in both few-shot and full-data settings. The source code can be found at here.

</details>


### [113] [RaTA-Tool: Retrieval-based Tool Selection with Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14951)
*Gabriele Mattioli,Evelyn Turri,Sara Sarto,Lorenzo Baraldi,Marcella Cornia,Lorenzo Baraldi,Rita Cucchiara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RaTA-Tool是一个用于开放世界多模态工具选择的框架，通过将多模态查询转换为结构化任务描述，然后与机器可读的工具描述进行语义匹配来检索最合适的工具，支持无需重新训练即可扩展到新工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用方法主要局限于纯文本输入和封闭世界设置，难以解释多模态用户指令，且无法泛化到训练期间未见过的工具。需要解决开放世界多模态工具选择的问题。

Method: 提出RaTA-Tool框架：1) 使用MLLM将多模态查询转换为结构化任务描述；2) 通过语义匹配将该表示与机器可读的工具描述进行检索；3) 使用直接偏好优化(DPO)进行偏好优化以提高任务描述与工具选择的对齐度。

Result: 实验表明该方法显著提高了工具选择性能，特别是在开放世界多模态场景中。还创建了首个开放世界多模态工具使用数据集，包含基于Hugging Face模型卡的标准化工具有描述。

Conclusion: RaTA-Tool通过检索式框架解决了开放世界多模态工具选择问题，支持工具扩展而无需重新训练，在工具选择性能上取得显著改进。

Abstract: Tool learning with foundation models aims to endow AI systems with the ability to invoke external resources -- such as APIs, computational utilities, and specialized models -- to solve complex tasks beyond the reach of standalone language generation. While recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) and Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have expanded their reasoning and perception capabilities, existing tool-use methods are predominantly limited to text-only inputs and closed-world settings. Consequently, they struggle to interpret multimodal user instructions and cannot generalize to tools unseen during training. In this work, we introduce RaTA-Tool, a novel framework for open-world multimodal tool selection. Rather than learning direct mappings from user queries to fixed tool identifiers, our approach enables an MLLM to convert a multimodal query into a structured task description and subsequently retrieve the most appropriate tool by matching this representation against semantically rich, machine-readable tool descriptions. This retrieval-based formulation naturally supports extensibility to new tools without retraining. To further improve alignment between task descriptions and tool selection, we incorporate a preference-based optimization stage using Direct Preference Optimization (DPO). To support research in this setting, we also introduce the first dataset for open-world multimodal tool use, featuring standardized tool descriptions derived from Hugging Face model cards. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly improves tool-selection performance, particularly in open-world, multimodal scenarios.

</details>


### [114] [Prompt-to-Gesture: Measuring the Capabilities of Image-to-Video Deictic Gesture Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14953)
*Hassan Ali,Doreen Jirak,Luca Müller,Stefan Wermter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出使用图像到视频生成模型创建逼真的指示性手势数据集，以解决手势识别领域数据稀缺问题，并验证合成数据对下游任务的增强效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手势识别研究面临严重的数据稀缺问题，传统方法依赖昂贵的人工录制或无法生成真实手势变化的图像处理方法。图像到视频基础模型的最新进展为创建免人工的合成数据提供了新可能，但需要验证这些生成模型能否有效补充传统人类生成的手势数据。

Method: 提出基于提示的视频生成方法构建现实的指示性手势数据集。开发了一个数据生成流程，从少量人类参与者收集的参考样本中生成指示性手势，提供了一种可在机器学习社区内外使用的可访问方法。

Result: 合成手势不仅在视觉保真度上与真实手势高度一致，还引入了有意义的变化和新颖性，丰富了原始数据。使用混合数据集的各种深度模型表现出优越性能，进一步支持了合成数据的有效性。

Conclusion: 图像到视频技术即使处于早期阶段，也为手势合成提供了强大的零样本方法，对下游任务具有明显益处。该方法为解决手势识别数据稀缺问题提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Gesture recognition research, unlike NLP, continues to face acute data scarcity, with progress constrained by the need for costly human recordings or image processing approaches that cannot generate authentic variability in the gestures themselves. Recent advancements in image-to-video foundation models have enabled the generation of photorealistic, semantically rich videos guided by natural language. These capabilities open up new possibilities for creating effort-free synthetic data, raising the critical question of whether video Generative AI models can augment and complement traditional human-generated gesture data. In this paper, we introduce and analyze prompt-based video generation to construct a realistic deictic gestures dataset and rigorously evaluate its effectiveness for downstream tasks. We propose a data generation pipeline that produces deictic gestures from a small number of reference samples collected from human participants, providing an accessible approach that can be leveraged both within and beyond the machine learning community. Our results demonstrate that the synthetic gestures not only align closely with real ones in terms of visual fidelity but also introduce meaningful variability and novelty that enrich the original data, further supported by superior performance of various deep models using a mixed dataset. These findings highlight that image-to-video techniques, even in their early stages, offer a powerful zero-shot approach to gesture synthesis with clear benefits for downstream tasks.

</details>


### [115] [UniDoc-RL: Coarse-to-Fine Visual RAG with Hierarchical Actions and Dense Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14967)
*Jun Wang,Shuo Tan,Zelong Sun,Tiancheng Gu,Yongle Zhao,Ziyong Feng,Kaicheng Yang,Cewu Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniDoc-RL：一个统一的强化学习框架，通过分层动作空间让大型视觉语言模型联合执行检索、重排序、主动视觉感知和推理，显著提升视觉RAG系统的细粒度语义理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉RAG系统依赖通用检索信号，忽略了复杂推理所需的细粒度视觉语义，导致检索内容不够精确，影响推理质量。

Method: 提出UniDoc-RL框架，将视觉信息获取建模为具有分层动作空间的序列决策问题：从粗粒度文档检索到细粒度图像选择，再到主动区域裁剪。采用基于Group Relative Policy Optimization的端到端训练，引入密集多奖励方案为每个动作提供任务感知监督。

Result: 在三个基准测试中，UniDoc-RL持续超越现有最先进基线，相比之前的基于RL的方法提升高达17.7%。

Conclusion: UniDoc-RL通过统一的强化学习框架有效解决了视觉RAG中细粒度语义理解不足的问题，实现了从粗到精的视觉证据渐进式提炼，显著提升了复杂视觉推理任务的性能。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) extends Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) with external visual knowledge. However, existing visual RAG systems typically rely on generic retrieval signals that overlook the fine-grained visual semantics essential for complex reasoning. To address this limitation, we propose UniDoc-RL, a unified reinforcement learning framework in which an LVLM agent jointly performs retrieval, reranking, active visual perception, and reasoning. UniDoc-RL formulates visual information acquisition as a sequential decision-making problem with a hierarchical action space. Specifically, it progressively refines visual evidence from coarse-grained document retrieval to fine-grained image selection and active region cropping, allowing the model to suppress irrelevant content and attend to information-dense regions. For effective end-to-end training, we introduce a dense multi-reward scheme that provides task-aware supervision for each action. Based on Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), UniDoc-RL aligns agent behavior with multiple objectives without relying on a separate value network. To support this training paradigm, we curate a comprehensive dataset of high-quality reasoning trajectories with fine-grained action annotations. Experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate that UniDoc-RL consistently surpasses state-of-the-art baselines, yielding up to 17.7% gains over prior RL-based methods.

</details>


### [116] [Flow of Truth: Proactive Temporal Forensics for Image-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15003)
*Yuzhuo Chen,Zehua Ma,Han Fang,Hengyi Wang,Guanjie Wang,Weiming Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首个专注于图像到视频生成中时间维度取证的前瞻性框架，通过像素运动追踪而非传统空间取证来检测I2V生成内容


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像到视频生成技术的快速发展带来了新的取证需求，传统2D像素级篡改定位方法无法处理视频中像素随时间的流动和变形，需要开发能够追踪像素在整个视频中如何流动和变换的时间维度取证方法

Method: 创新性地将视频生成重新定义为"像素随时间运动而非帧的合成"，提出可学习的取证模板跟随像素运动，以及模板引导的流模块将运动与图像内容解耦，实现鲁棒的时间追踪

Result: Flow of Truth框架在商业和开源I2V模型中均表现出良好的泛化能力，显著提升了时间维度取证性能

Conclusion: 该研究填补了I2V生成时间维度取证的空白，通过像素运动视角重新定义视频生成，为动态内容取证提供了新范式

Abstract: The rapid rise of image-to-video (I2V) generation enables realistic videos to be created from a single image but also brings new forensic demands. Unlike static images, I2V content evolves over time, requiring forensics to move beyond 2D pixel-level tampering localization toward tracing how pixels flow and transform throughout the video. As frames progress, embedded traces drift and deform, making traditional spatial forensics ineffective. To address this unexplored dimension, we present **Flow of Truth**, the first proactive framework focusing on temporal forensics in I2V generation. A key challenge lies in discovering a forensic signature that can evolve consistently with the generation process, which is inherently a creative transformation rather than a deterministic reconstruction. Despite this intrinsic difficulty, we innovatively redefine video generation as *the motion of pixels through time rather than the synthesis of frames*. Building on this view, we propose a learnable forensic template that follows pixel motion and a template-guided flow module that decouples motion from image content, enabling robust temporal tracing. Experiments show that Flow of Truth generalizes across commercial and open-source I2V models, substantially improving temporal forensics performance.

</details>


### [117] [Attention-Gated Convolutional Networks for Scanner-Agnostic Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15059)
*Chinmay Bakhale,Anil Sao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出混合CNN-注意力框架用于MRI质量评估，在可见站点达到0.9920准确率，在未见站点保持0.755准确率，无需重新训练即可抵抗域偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动伪影严重影响结构MRI的临床诊断和自动化分析，手动质量控制在大规模纵向研究中不可扩展，需要开发能够跨站点泛化的自动质量评估方法。

Method: 提出混合CNN-注意力框架：使用分层2D CNN编码器提取局部空间特征，结合多头交叉注意力机制建模全局依赖关系，动态过滤站点特异性强度变化和背景噪声，专注于运动相关伪影特征。

Result: 在MR-ART数据集上训练，可见站点评估准确率0.9920，F1分数0.9919；在未见ABIDE站点（17个异质站点）评估准确率0.755，无需重新训练或微调，表现出对域偏移的高鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 注意力机制的特征重加权成功捕获了通用伪影描述符，缩小了不同成像环境和扫描仪制造商之间的性能差距，为大规模MRI研究提供了可扩展的质量控制解决方案。

Abstract: Motion artifacts present a significant challenge in structural MRI (sMRI), often compromising clinical diagnostics and large-scale automated analysis. While manual quality control (QC) remains the gold standard, it is increasingly unscalable for massive longitudinal studies. To address this, we propose a hybrid CNN-Attention framework designed for robust, site-invariant MRI quality assessment. Our architecture integrates a hierarchical 2D CNN encoder for local spatial feature extraction with a multi-head cross-attention mechanism to model global dependencies. This synergy enables the model to prioritize motion relevant artifact signatures, such as ringing and blurring, while dynamically filtering out site-specific intensity variations and background noise. The framework was trained end-to-end on the MR-ART dataset using a balanced cohort of 200 subjects. Performance was evaluated across two tiers: Seen Site Evaluation on a held-out MR-ART partition and Unseen Site Evaluation using 200 subjects from 17 heterogeneous sites in the ABIDE archive. On seen sites, the model achieved a scan-level accuracy of 0.9920 and an F1-score of 0.9919. Crucially, it maintained strong generalization across unseen ABIDE sites (Acc = 0.755) without any retraining or fine-tuning, demonstrating high resilience to domain shift. These results indicate that attention-based feature re-weighting successfully captures universal artifact descriptors, bridging the performance gap between diverse imaging environments and scanner manufacturers.

</details>


### [118] [Learning Where to Embed: Noise-Aware Positional Embedding for Query Retrieval in Small-Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15065)
*Yangchen Zeng,Zhenyu Yu,Dongming Jiang,Wenbo Zhang,Yifan Hong,Zhanhua Hu,Jiao Luo,Kangning Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HELP框架通过热图引导的位置嵌入和梯度掩码过滤，在减少解码器层数和参数量的同时提升小目标检测性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的小目标检测器效率低下且易受背景噪声干扰，需要深度解码器来细化低质量查询

Method: 提出HELP框架，包含热图引导的位置嵌入(HPE)机制，在编码器和解码器中选择性保留前景区域的位置编码并抑制背景噪声，使用梯度掩码过滤背景主导嵌入，集成线性蛇形卷积丰富特征表示

Result: 解码器层数从8层减少到3层，参数量减少59.4%(66.3M vs 163M)，在减少计算预算的同时保持准确率提升

Conclusion: HELP框架通过噪声感知的位置-语义融合机制，在显著降低模型复杂度的同时有效提升小目标检测性能

Abstract: Transformer-based detectors have advanced small-object detection, but they often remain inefficient and vulnerable to background-induced query noise, which motivates deep decoders to refine low-quality queries. We present HELP (Heatmap-guided Embedding Learning Paradigm), a noise-aware positional-semantic fusion framework that studies where to embed positional information by selectively preserving positional encodings in foreground-salient regions while suppressing background clutter. Within HELP, we introduce Heatmap-guided Positional Embedding (HPE) as the core embedding mechanism and visualize it with a heatbar for interpretable diagnosis and fine-tuning. HPE is integrated into both the encoder and decoder: it guides noise-suppressed feature encoding by injecting heatmap-aware positional encoding, and it enables high-quality query retrieval by filtering background-dominant embeddings via a gradient-based mask filter before decoding. To address feature sparsity in complex small targets, we integrate Linear-Snake Convolution to enrich retrieval-relevant representations. The gradient-based heatmap supervision is used during training only, incurring no additional gradient computation at inference. As a result, our design reduces decoder layers from eight to three and achieves a 59.4% parameter reduction (66.3M vs. 163M) while maintaining consistent accuracy gains under a reduced compute budget across benchmarks. Code Repository: https://github.com/yidimopozhibai/Noise-Suppressed-Query-Retrieval

</details>


### [119] [Building Extraction from Remote Sensing Imagery under Hazy and Low-light Conditions: Benchmark and Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15088)
*Feifei Sang,Wei Lu,Hongruixuan Chen,Sibao Chen,Bin Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首个针对雾霾和低光条件下建筑物提取的光学遥感基准数据集HaLoBuilding及相应端到端框架HaLoBuild-Net，通过空间-频率聚焦模块和全局多尺度引导模块有效缓解气象干扰，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光学遥感建筑物提取方法主要针对理想晴朗天气条件，在真实世界的雾霾和低光条件下性能严重下降。虽然SAR具备全天候感知能力，但其侧视几何结构会导致几何畸变。需要专门针对恶劣天气条件的建筑物提取解决方案。

Method: 提出HaLoBuilding基准数据集，采用同场景多时相对策略确保像素级标签对齐和高保真度。提出HaLoBuild-Net端到端框架：1) 空间-频率聚焦模块(SFFM)通过大感受野注意力与频率感知通道重加权结合稳定低频锚点来缓解气象干扰；2) 全局多尺度引导模块(GMGM)提供全局语义约束锚定建筑物拓扑；3) 互引导融合模块(MGFM)实现双向语义-空间校准以抑制浅层噪声和锐化天气引起的模糊边界。

Result: 在HaLoBuilding数据集上，HaLoBuild-Net显著优于现有最先进方法和传统的级联恢复-分割范式。同时在WHU、INRIA和LoveDA数据集上保持了强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: HaLoBuilding是首个专门针对雾霾和低光条件下建筑物提取的光学遥感基准数据集，HaLoBuild-Net框架通过创新的空间-频率聚焦和全局语义引导机制，有效解决了恶劣天气条件下的建筑物提取问题，为实际遥感应用提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: Building extraction from optical Remote Sensing (RS) imagery suffers from performance degradation under real-world hazy and low-light conditions. However, existing optical methods and benchmarks focus primarily on ideal clear-weather conditions. While SAR offers all-weather sensing, its side-looking geometry causes geometric distortions. To address these challenges, we introduce HaLoBuilding, the first optical benchmark specifically designed for building extraction under hazy and low-light conditions. By leveraging a same-scene multitemporal pairing strategy, we ensure pixel-level label alignment and high fidelity even under extreme degradation. Building upon this benchmark, we propose HaLoBuild-Net, a novel end-to-end framework for building extraction in adverse RS scenarios. At its core, we develop a Spatial-Frequency Focus Module (SFFM) to effectively mitigate meteorological interference on building features by coupling large receptive field attention with frequency-aware channel reweighting guided by stable low-frequency anchors. Additionally, a Global Multi-scale Guidance Module (GMGM) provides global semantic constraints to anchor building topologies, while a Mutual-Guided Fusion Module (MGFM) implements bidirectional semantic-spatial calibration to suppress shallow noise and sharpen weather-induced blurred boundaries. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HaLoBuild-Net significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods and conventional cascaded restoration-segmentation paradigms on the HaLoBuilding dataset, while maintaining robust generalization on WHU, INRIA, and LoveDA datasets. The source code and datasets are publicly available at: https://github.com/AeroVILab-AHU/HaLoBuilding.

</details>


### [120] [Beyond Visual Cues: Semantic-Driven Token Filtering and Expert Routing for Anytime Person ReID](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15090)
*Jiaxuan Li,Xin Wen,Zhihang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出STFER框架，利用大视觉语言模型生成身份一致性文本，通过语义驱动视觉令牌过滤和专家路由，解决任意时间行人重识别中的模态偏移和衣物变化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法过度依赖纯视觉特征，容易受环境和时间因素影响，在光照引起的模态偏移或衣物变化场景下性能显著下降。需要一种能够同时应对衣物变化和RGB-IR跨模态偏移的鲁棒身份识别方法。

Method: 提出语义驱动令牌过滤和专家路由（STFER）框架：1）使用指令引导LVLM生成身份内在语义文本，捕捉生物特征常量；2）语义驱动视觉令牌过滤（SVTF）利用文本令牌增强信息性视觉区域，抑制冗余背景噪声；3）语义驱动专家路由（SER）将语义文本集成到专家路由中，实现更鲁棒的多场景门控。

Result: 在AT-USTC数据集上取得最先进结果，同时在5个广泛使用的ReID基准测试中表现出优越的泛化能力和高度竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: STFER框架通过利用LVLM生成的身份一致性文本，有效解决了任意时间行人重识别中的衣物变化和跨模态偏移问题，展示了强大的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Any-Time Person Re-identification (AT-ReID) necessitates the robust retrieval of target individuals under arbitrary conditions, encompassing both modality shifts (daytime and nighttime) and extensive clothing-change scenarios, ranging from short-term to long-term intervals. However, existing methods are highly relying on pure visual features, which are prone to change due to environmental and time factors, resulting in significantly performance deterioration under scenarios involving illumination caused modality shifts or cloth-change. In this paper, we propose Semantic-driven Token Filtering and Expert Routing (STFER), a novel framework that leverages the ability of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) to generate identity consistency text, which provides identity-discriminative features that are robust to both clothing variations and cross-modality shifts between RGB and IR. Specifically, we employ instructions to guide the LVLM in generating identity-intrinsic semantic text that captures biometric constants for the semantic model driven. The text token is further used for Semantic-driven Visual Token Filtering (SVTF), which enhances informative visual regions and suppresses redundant background noise. Meanwhile, the text token is also used for Semantic-driven Expert Routing (SER), which integrates the semantic text into expert routing, resulting in more robust multi-scenario gating. Extensive experiments on the Any-Time ReID dataset (AT-USTC) demonstrate that our model achieves state-of-the-art results. Moreover, the model trained on AT-USTC was evaluated across 5 widely-used ReID benchmarks demonstrating superior generalization capabilities with highly competitive results. Our code will be available soon.

</details>


### [121] [Beyond Independent Frames: Latent Attention Masked Autoencoders for Multi-View Echocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15096)
*Simon Böhi,Irene Cannistraci,Sergio Muñoz Gonzalez,Moritz Vandenhirtz,Sonia Laguna,Samuel Ruiperez-Campillo,Max Krähenmann,Andrea Agostini,Ece Ozkan,Thomas M. Sutter,Julia E. Vogt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LAMAE是一种针对医学影像多视角特性设计的掩码自编码器基础模型，通过潜在注意力模块实现跨帧跨视图信息交换，在MIMIC-IV-ECHO数据集上预训练，首次实现从超声心动图视频预测ICD-10编码，并展示良好的跨年龄组迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声心动图因其无创和经济性而广泛应用，但其稀疏且异质性的时空视图给心脏评估带来挑战。现有掩码自编码器方法通常独立处理图像或短片段，无法捕捉心脏表示所需的多视图结构。

Method: 提出潜在注意力掩码自编码器（LAMAE），在标准MAE基础上增加潜在注意力模块，允许在潜在空间中直接进行跨帧和跨视图的信息交换。该方法能够聚合可变长度序列和不同视图，从部分观测重建心脏功能的整体表示。在MIMIC-IV-ECHO大规模非精选数据集上进行预训练。

Result: 首次展示了从MIMIC-IV-ECHO视频预测ICD-10编码的结果。实验证明，从成人数据学习的表征能够有效迁移到儿科队列，尽管存在显著的解剖学差异。这表明融入多视图注意力等结构先验能够产生更鲁棒和可迁移的表征。

Conclusion: LAMAE通过引入潜在注意力模块专门针对医学影像的多视角特性，能够从部分观测中重建心脏功能的整体表示。该方法在MIMIC-IV-ECHO数据集上表现出色，首次实现从超声心动图视频预测ICD-10编码，并展示了良好的跨年龄组迁移能力，证明了结构先验对提升表征鲁棒性和可迁移性的重要性。

Abstract: Echocardiography is a widely used modality for cardiac assessment due to its non-invasive and cost-effective nature, but the sparse and heterogeneous spatiotemporal views of the heart pose distinct challenges. Existing masked autoencoder (MAE) approaches typically process images or short clips independently, failing to capture the inherent multi-view structure required for coherent cardiac representation. We introduce Latent Attention Masked Autoencoder (LAMAE), a foundation model architecture tailored to the multi-view nature of medical imaging. LAMAE augments the standard MAE with a latent attention module that enables information exchange across frames and views directly in latent space. This allows the model to aggregate variable-length sequences and distinct views, reconstructing a holistic representation of cardiac function from partial observations. We pretrain LAMAE on MIMIC-IV-ECHO, a large-scale, uncurated dataset reflecting real-world clinical variability. To the best of our knowledge, we present the first results for predicting ICD-10 codes from MIMIC-IV-ECHO videos. Furthermore, we empirically demonstrate that representations learned from adult data transfer effectively to pediatric cohorts despite substantial anatomical differences. These results provide evidence that incorporating structural priors, such as multi-view attention, yields significantly more robust and transferable representations.

</details>


### [122] [How to Correctly Make Mistakes: A Framework for Constructing and Benchmarking Mistake Aware Egocentric Procedural Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15134)
*Olga Loginova,Frank Keller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PIE-V框架通过心理学启发的错误注入和修正规划，为第一人称视角程序视频构建错误感知数据集，支持程序错误检测与修正的基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有程序视频数据集缺乏自然发生的人类错误和恢复行为的系统记录，而第一人称视角中错误常被手部遮挡且通过细微物体状态变化显现，需要构建包含可控、人类可信偏差的程序视频基准。

Method: PIE-V框架包含：1) 基于程序阶段和语义步骤负载的心理学启发错误规划器；2) 建模恢复行为的修正规划器；3) 执行级联一致重写的LLM编写器；4) 验证程序连贯性和修复失败的LLM评判器；5) 使用文本引导视频生成合成替换片段并缝合到原始视频中。

Result: 应用于17个任务和50个Ego-Exo4D场景，注入102个错误并生成27个恢复修正；建立了包含9个指标的统一定义和人工评估标准，涵盖步骤级和程序级质量；审计了现有资源并与自由形式LLM生成基线进行比较。

Conclusion: PIE-V框架和评估标准支持第一人称视角程序错误检测与修正的事后验证，为程序监控提供了系统化的错误注入和评估方法。

Abstract: Reliable procedural monitoring in video requires exposure to naturally occurring human errors and the recoveries that follow. In egocentric recordings, mistakes are often partially occluded by hands and revealed through subtle object state changes, while existing procedural datasets provide limited and inconsistent mistake and correction traces. We present PIE-V (Psychologically Inspired Error injection for Videos), a framework for constructing and benchmarking mistake-aware egocentric procedural videos by augmenting clean keystep procedures with controlled, human-plausible deviations. PIE-V combines a psychology-informed error planner conditioned on procedure phase and semantic step load, a correction planner that models recovery behavior, an LLM writer that performs cascade-consistent rewrites, and an LLM judge that validates procedural coherence and repairs failures. For video segment edits, PIE-V synthesizes replacement clips with text-guided video generation and stitches them into the episode to preserve visual plausibility. Applied to 17 tasks and 50 Ego-Exo4D scenarios, PIE-V injects 102 mistakes and generates 27 recovery corrections. For benchmarking, we introduce a unified taxonomy and a human rubric with nine metrics that cover step-level and procedure-level quality, including plausibility, procedure logic with annotator confidence, state change coherence, and grounding between text and video. Using this protocol, we audit several existing resources and compare PIE-V against a freeform LLM generation baseline under the same criteria. Together, the framework and rubric support post-completion verification for egocentric procedural mistake detection and correction.

</details>


### [123] [Class Unlearning via Depth-Aware Removal of Forget-Specific Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15166)
*Arman Hatami,Romina Aalishah,Ilya E. Monosov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DAMP是一种无需梯度优化的单次权重手术方法，通过投影调制深度感知地移除遗忘类特定方向，实现选择性遗忘同时保留保留类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类别遗忘方法存在局限性：遗忘类准确率降低不一定代表真正遗忘，遗忘信息可能仍编码在内部表示中，表面遗忘可能源于分类头抑制而非表示移除。现有方法常表现出弱或负选择性，在深层表示中保留遗忘类结构，或过度依赖最终层偏置偏移。

Method: DAMP（深度感知投影调制）是一种单次闭式权重手术方法。在每个阶段，计算下一可学习算子输入空间中的类别原型，提取遗忘方向作为相对于保留类原型的残差，应用基于投影的更新以减少对这些方向的下游敏感性。为保持效用，使用基于探针可分离性导出的无参数深度感知缩放规则，在早期层应用较小编辑，在深层应用较大编辑。方法自然扩展到通过低秩子空间移除实现多类遗忘。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和Tiny ImageNet数据集上，以及卷积和Transformer架构中，DAMP比现有方法更接近重新训练的黄金标准，改善了选择性遗忘，同时更好地保留了保留类性能，并减少了深层中残留的遗忘类结构。

Conclusion: DAMP通过深度感知投影调制提供了一种有效的类别遗忘方法，能够真正移除遗忘类特定方向而非仅抑制分类头，在选择性遗忘和保留性能之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove targeted knowledge from a trained model without the cost of retraining from scratch. In class unlearning, however, reducing accuracy on forget classes does not necessarily imply true forgetting: forgotten information can remain encoded in internal representations, and apparent forgetting may arise from classifier-head suppression rather than representational removal. We show that existing class-unlearning methods often exhibit weak or negative selectivity, preserve forget-class structure in deep representations, or rely heavily on final-layer bias shifts. We then introduce DAMP (Depth-Aware Modulation by Projection), a one-shot, closed-form weight-surgery method that removes forget-specific directions from a pretrained network without gradient-based optimization. At each stage, DAMP computes class prototypes in the input space of the next learnable operator, extracts forget directions as residuals relative to retain-class prototypes, and applies a projection-based update to reduce downstream sensitivity to those directions. To preserve utility, DAMP uses a parameter-free depth-aware scaling rule derived from probe separability, applying smaller edits in early layers and larger edits in deeper layers. The method naturally extends to multi-class forgetting through low-rank subspace removal. Across MNIST, CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Tiny ImageNet, and across convolutional and transformer architectures, DAMP more closely resembles the retraining gold standard than some of the prior methods, improving selective forgetting while better preserving retain-class performance and reducing residual forget-class structure in deep layers.

</details>


### [124] [OmniLight: One Model to Rule All Lighting Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15170)
*Youngjin Oh,Junyoung Park,Junhyeong Kwon,Nam Ik Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究了光照相关图像恢复问题，提出了两种对比策略：基于DINOLight的专用架构和基于WD-MoE的统一架构OmniLight，在NTIRE 2026挑战赛中均取得顶级排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不良光照条件（如投影阴影和不规则照明）会降低计算机视觉系统的可见度和色彩保真度，影响下游任务性能。虽然特定基准测试的SOTA结果是主要目标，但实际应用需要能够处理多样化领域的鲁棒模型。

Method: 提出两种对比策略：1）使用DINOLight作为专用基线，针对每个数据集特性进行优化；2）扩展为OmniLight，采用提出的小波域专家混合（WD-MoE）架构，在所有数据集上联合训练，实现统一解决方案。

Result: 两种方法在NTIRE 2026挑战赛的三个光照相关赛道中均获得顶级排名，展示了出色的感知质量和泛化能力。通过比较分析，探讨了数据分布对专用和统一架构性能的影响。

Conclusion: 该研究为光照相关图像恢复提供了全面的分析框架，展示了专用和统一架构各自的优势，为解决实际应用中的多样化光照问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Adverse lighting conditions, such as cast shadows and irregular illumination, pose significant challenges to computer vision systems by degrading visibility and color fidelity. Consequently, effective shadow removal and ALN are critical for restoring underlying image content, improving perceptual quality, and facilitating robust performance in downstream tasks. However, while achieving state-of-the-art results on specific benchmarks is a primary goal in image restoration challenges, real-world applications often demand robust models capable of handling diverse domains. To address this, we present a comprehensive study on lighting-related image restoration by exploring two contrasting strategies. We leverage a robust framework for ALN, DINOLight, as a specialized baseline to exploit the characteristics of each individual dataset, and extend it to OmniLight, a generalized alternative incorporating our proposed Wavelet Domain Mixture-of-Experts (WD-MoE) that is trained across all provided datasets. Through a comparative analysis of these two methods, we discuss the impact of data distribution on the performance of specialized and unified architectures in lighting-related image restoration. Notably, both approaches secured top-tier rankings across all three lighting-related tracks in the NTIRE 2026 Challenge, demonstrating their outstanding perceptual quality and generalization capabilities. Our codes are available at https://github.com/OBAKSA/Lighting-Restoration.

</details>


### [125] [Boundary-Centric Active Learning for Temporal Action Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15173)
*Halil Ismail Helvaci,Sen-ching Samson Cheung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: B-ACT是一种面向时序动作分割的主动学习框架，通过边界中心化标注策略，在有限标注预算下显著提升模型性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序动作分割需要密集的时间标注，但大部分标注成本都花费在识别和精修动作边界上，这些区域正是分割错误集中且微小时间偏移会显著降低分段指标的地方

Method: 提出B-ACT框架，采用分层两阶段循环：1)基于预测不确定性对未标注视频排序和查询；2)在选中的视频中，从当前模型预测中检测候选边界，通过融合邻域不确定性、类别模糊性和时序预测动态的新边界评分选择top-K边界。标注协议仅请求边界帧的标签，但仍使用边界中心的剪辑进行训练以利用模型感受野的时序上下文

Result: 在GTEA、50Salads和Breakfast数据集上的广泛实验表明，边界中心化监督实现了强大的标签效率，在稀疏预算下持续超越代表性TAS主动学习基线和先前最先进方法，在边界放置主导编辑和基于重叠的F1分数的数据集上获得最大增益

Conclusion: B-ACT框架通过明确将监督分配到高影响力的边界区域，有效解决了时序动作分割中的标注效率问题，为资源受限的场景提供了实用的解决方案

Abstract: Temporal action segmentation (TAS) demands dense temporal supervision, yet most of the annotation cost in untrimmed videos is spent identifying and refining action transitions, where segmentation errors concentrate and small temporal shifts disproportionately degrade segmental metrics. We introduce B-ACT, a clip-budgeted active learning framework that explicitly allocates supervision to these high-leverage boundary regions. B-ACT operates in a hierarchical two-stage loop: (i) it ranks and queries unlabeled videos using predictive uncertainty, and (ii) within each selected video, it detects candidate transitions from the current model predictions and selects the top-$K$ boundaries via a novel boundary score that fuses neighborhood uncertainty, class ambiguity, and temporal predictive dynamics. Importantly, our annotation protocol requests labels for only the boundary frames while still training on boundary-centered clips to exploit temporal context through the model's receptive field. Extensive experiments on GTEA, 50Salads, and Breakfast demonstrate that boundary-centric supervision delivers strong label efficiency and consistently surpasses representative TAS active learning baselines and prior state of the art under sparse budgets, with the largest gains on datasets where boundary placement dominates edit and overlap-based F1 scores.

</details>


### [126] [VisPCO: Visual Token Pruning Configuration Optimization via Budget-Aware Pareto-Frontier Learning for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15188)
*Huawei Ji,Yuanhao Sun,Yuan Jin,Cheng Deng,Jiaxin Ding,Luoyi Fu,Xinbing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个名为（名称未明确）的新框架，将视觉令牌剪枝建模为帕累托配置优化问题，通过连续松弛和直通估计器实现梯度搜索，自动寻找计算-性能最优配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉令牌剪枝方法依赖预定义配置，无法保证达到计算-性能最优平衡。需要自动寻找最优剪枝配置的方法。

Method: 将视觉令牌剪枝建模为帕累托配置优化问题，采用连续松弛和直通估计器实现梯度搜索，通过增广拉格朗日法求解。引入可学习核函数研究分层剪枝模式。

Result: 在8个视觉基准测试中，该方法能有效逼近网格搜索得到的经验帕累托前沿，在不同剪枝方法和VLM架构上泛化良好。多步渐进剪枝比单层方法获得更优的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 该框架能自动寻找视觉令牌剪枝的最优配置，揭示了多步渐进剪枝能捕捉VLM的分层压缩结构，为视觉语言模型的高效处理提供了新方法。

Abstract: Visual token pruning methods effectively mitigate the quadratic computational growth caused by processing high-resolution images and video frames in vision-language models (VLMs). However, existing approaches rely on predefined pruning configurations without determining whether they achieve computation-performance optimality. In this work, we introduce , a novel framework that formulates visual token pruning as a Pareto configuration optimization problem to automatically identify optimal configurations. Our approach employs continuous relaxation and straight-through estimators to enable gradient-based search, solved via the Augmented Lagrangian method. Extensive experiments across 8 visual benchmarks demonstrate that effectively approximates the empirical Pareto frontier obtained through grid search and generalizes well across various pruning methods and VLM architectures. Furthermore, through learnable kernel functions, we investigate layer-wise pruning patterns and reveal that multi-step progressive pruning captures VLMs' hierarchical compression structure, achieving superior accuracy-efficiency trade-offs compared to single-layer approaches.

</details>


### [127] [Unsupervised Skeleton-Based Action Segmentation via Hierarchical Spatiotemporal Vector Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15196)
*Umer Ahmed,Syed Ahmed Mahmood,Fawad Javed Fateh,M. Shaheer Luqman,M. Zeeshan Zia,Quoc-Huy Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于无监督骨架时序动作分割的分层时空向量量化框架，通过两级向量量化实现从细粒度子动作到动作级表示的聚合，并在多个基准测试中达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无监督骨架时序动作分割中的挑战，特别是如何有效利用骨架数据的空间和时间信息，同时减少分割长度偏差。现有方法通常缺乏对动作层次结构的建模，且未能充分利用时空信息。

Method: 1. 分层向量量化：包含两个连续级别的向量量化，底层将骨架关联到细粒度子动作，高层将子动作聚合为动作级表示。2. 分层时空向量量化：扩展方法以同时利用空间和时间信息，通过多级聚类同时恢复输入骨架及其对应时间戳。3. 通过重建输入骨架和时间戳进行无监督学习。

Result: 在HuGaDB、LARa和BABEL等多个基准测试上建立了新的最先进性能，分层方法优于非分层基线，同时减少了无监督骨架时序动作分割中的分割长度偏差。

Conclusion: 提出的分层时空向量量化框架有效解决了无监督骨架时序动作分割问题，通过层次化建模和时空信息融合显著提升了性能，为这一领域提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a novel hierarchical spatiotemporal vector quantization framework for unsupervised skeleton-based temporal action segmentation. We first introduce a hierarchical approach, which includes two consecutive levels of vector quantization. Specifically, the lower level associates skeletons with fine-grained subactions, while the higher level further aggregates subactions into action-level representations. Our hierarchical approach outperforms the non-hierarchical baseline, while primarily exploiting spatial cues by reconstructing input skeletons. Next, we extend our approach by leveraging both spatial and temporal information, yielding a hierarchical spatiotemporal vector quantization scheme. In particular, our hierarchical spatiotemporal approach performs multi-level clustering, while simultaneously recovering input skeletons and their corresponding timestamps. Lastly, extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks, including HuGaDB, LARa, and BABEL, demonstrate that our approach establishes a new state-of-the-art performance and reduces segment length bias in unsupervised skeleton-based temporal action segmentation.

</details>


### [128] [StreamCacheVGGT: Streaming Visual Geometry Transformers with Robust Scoring and Hybrid Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15237)
*Xuanyi Liu,Deyi Ji,Chunan Yu,Qi Zhu,Xuanfu Li,Jin Ma,Tianrun Chen,Lanyun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamCacheVGGT：一种无需训练的视频流3D重建框架，通过跨层一致性增强评分和混合缓存压缩解决现有O(1)方法的信息破坏问题，在多个基准测试中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有O(1)内存框架主要依赖"纯淘汰"范式，存在信息破坏严重的问题，原因包括二元令牌删除和局部单层评分带来的评估噪声。需要一种能保持几何上下文、提升稳定性的缓存管理方法。

Method: 提出StreamCacheVGGT框架，包含两个协同模块：1) 跨层一致性增强评分(CLCES)：通过跟踪Transformer层次结构中令牌重要性轨迹，使用顺序统计分析识别持续几何显著性；2) 混合缓存压缩(HCC)：采用三级分类策略，将中等重要性令牌通过键向量流形上的最近邻分配合并到保留锚点中，避免简单淘汰。

Result: 在7-Scenes、NRGBD、ETH3D、Bonn和KITTI五个基准测试上进行广泛评估，StreamCacheVGGT实现了新的最先进性能，在严格遵循恒定成本约束的同时，提供了优越的重建精度和长期稳定性。

Conclusion: StreamCacheVGGT通过重新构想缓存管理，解决了现有O(1)框架的信息破坏瓶颈，通过跨层一致性评分和混合压缩策略，在恒定内存预算下实现了更稳定、更准确的密集3D几何重建。

Abstract: Reconstructing dense 3D geometry from continuous video streams requires stable inference under a constant memory budget. Existing $O(1)$ frameworks primarily rely on a ``pure eviction'' paradigm, which suffers from significant information destruction due to binary token deletion and evaluation noise from localized, single-layer scoring. To address these bottlenecks, we propose StreamCacheVGGT, a training-free framework that reimagines cache management through two synergistic modules: Cross-Layer Consistency-Enhanced Scoring (CLCES) and Hybrid Cache Compression (HCC). CLCES mitigates activation noise by tracking token importance trajectories across the Transformer hierarchy, employing order-statistical analysis to identify sustained geometric salience. Leveraging these robust scores, HCC transcends simple eviction by introducing a three-tier triage strategy that merges moderately important tokens into retained anchors via nearest-neighbor assignment on the key-vector manifold. This approach preserves essential geometric context that would otherwise be lost. Extensive evaluations on five benchmarks (7-Scenes, NRGBD, ETH3D, Bonn, and KITTI) demonstrate that StreamCacheVGGT sets a new state-of-the-art, delivering superior reconstruction accuracy and long-term stability while strictly adhering to constant-cost constraints.

</details>


### [129] [TokenGS: Decoupling 3D Gaussian Prediction from Pixels with Learnable Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15239)
*Jiawei Ren,Michal Jan Tyszkiewicz,Jiahui Huang,Zan Gojcic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TokenGS：一种基于Transformer的编码器-解码器架构，通过可学习的高斯token直接回归3D均值坐标，改进前馈式3D高斯泼溅预测，实现更好的几何正则化和平衡分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的前馈式3D高斯泼溅预测方法通常沿相机光线回归高斯均值深度，这种方法存在局限性。作者认为直接回归3D均值坐标更优，且标准编码器架构限制了预测基元数量与输入图像分辨率和视图数量的绑定。

Method: 提出TokenGS方法：1) 放弃沿相机光线回归深度，改为直接回归3D均值坐标；2) 从编码器架构转向编码器-解码器架构；3) 引入可学习的高斯token，解绑预测基元数量与输入约束；4) 仅使用自监督渲染损失进行训练；5) 支持在token空间进行高效测试时优化。

Result: TokenGS在静态和动态场景的前馈重建中达到最先进性能：1) 对姿态噪声和多视图不一致性具有更强鲁棒性；2) 产生更正则化的几何结构和更平衡的3DGS分布；3) 自然恢复涌现的场景属性（如静态-动态分解和场景流）；4) 支持不降低学习先验的测试时优化。

Conclusion: 直接回归3D均值坐标的编码器-解码器架构优于传统的深度回归方法。TokenGS通过可学习高斯token实现了更灵活、鲁棒的前馈式3D高斯泼溅预测，为静态和动态场景重建提供了统一有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In this work, we revisit several key design choices of modern Transformer-based approaches for feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) prediction. We argue that the common practice of regressing Gaussian means as depths along camera rays is suboptimal, and instead propose to directly regress 3D mean coordinates using only a self-supervised rendering loss. This formulation allows us to move from the standard encoder-only design to an encoder-decoder architecture with learnable Gaussian tokens, thereby unbinding the number of predicted primitives from input image resolution and number of views. Our resulting method, TokenGS, demonstrates improved robustness to pose noise and multiview inconsistencies, while naturally supporting efficient test-time optimization in token space without degrading learned priors. TokenGS achieves state-of-the-art feed-forward reconstruction performance on both static and dynamic scenes, producing more regularized geometry and more balanced 3DGS distribution, while seamlessly recovering emergent scene attributes such as static-dynamic decomposition and scene flow.

</details>


### [130] [SegWithU: Uncertainty as Perturbation Energy for Single-Forward-Pass Risk-Aware Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15271)
*Tianhao Fu,Austin Wang,Charles Chen,Roby Aldave-Garza,Yucheng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SegWithU是一个用于医学图像分割的后处理不确定性估计框架，通过轻量级不确定性头增强冻结的预训练分割主干，在单次前向传播中提供两种体素级不确定性图，在多个数据集上实现了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割需要可靠的不确定性估计，因为自动化轮廓会影响下游量化和临床决策支持。现有方法要么需要重复推理效率低下，要么单次前向传播的替代方案提供较弱的质量或依赖限制性特征空间假设。

Method: SegWithU是一个后处理框架，通过轻量级不确定性头增强冻结的预训练分割主干。它利用中间主干特征，在紧凑的探测空间中使用秩-1后验探针将不确定性建模为扰动能量。框架生成两种体素级不确定性图：用于概率校准的校准导向图和用于错误检测与选择性预测的排序导向图。

Result: 在ACDC、BraTS2024和LiTS数据集上，SegWithU是性能最强且最一致的单一前向传播基线，分别实现了AUROC/AURC为0.9838/2.4885、0.9946/0.2660和0.9925/0.8193，同时保持了分割质量。

Conclusion: 基于扰动的不确定性建模是实现可靠性感知医学分割的有效且实用的途径。SegWithU框架在保持分割质量的同时，提供了高效可靠的不确定性估计。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty estimation is critical for medical image segmentation, where automated contours feed downstream quantification and clinical decision support. Many strong uncertainty methods require repeated inference, while efficient single-forward-pass alternatives often provide weaker failure ranking or rely on restrictive feature-space assumptions. We present $\textbf{SegWithU}$, a post-hoc framework that augments a frozen pretrained segmentation backbone with a lightweight uncertainty head. SegWithU taps intermediate backbone features and models uncertainty as perturbation energy in a compact probe space using rank-1 posterior probes. It produces two voxel-wise uncertainty maps: a calibration-oriented map for probability tempering and a ranking-oriented map for error detection and selective prediction. Across ACDC, BraTS2024, and LiTS, SegWithU is the strongest and most consistent single-forward-pass baseline, achieving AUROC/AURC of $0.9838/2.4885$, $0.9946/0.2660$, and $0.9925/0.8193$, respectively, while preserving segmentation quality. These results suggest that perturbation-based uncertainty modeling is an effective and practical route to reliability-aware medical segmentation.
  Source code is available at https://github.com/ProjectNeura/SegWithU.

</details>


### [131] [R3D: Revisiting 3D Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15281)
*Zhengdong Hong,Shenrui Wu,Haozhe Cui,Boyi Zhao,Ran Ji,Yiyang He,Hangxing Zhang,Zundong Ke,Jun Wang,Guofeng Zhang,Jiayuan Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出R3D-Policy：通过系统诊断3D策略学习中的训练不稳定和过拟合问题，引入3D数据增强和改进架构，实现稳定可扩展的3D模仿学习


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D策略学习具有优越的泛化能力和跨具身迁移潜力，但现有方法存在训练不稳定和严重过拟合问题，阻碍了强大3D感知模型的采用

Method: 系统诊断训练失败原因，识别出缺乏3D数据增强和批归一化的负面影响；提出新架构，将可扩展的基于Transformer的3D编码器与扩散解码器耦合，专门设计用于大规模稳定训练并利用大规模预训练

Result: 在具有挑战性的操作基准测试中显著优于最先进的3D基线方法，为可扩展的3D模仿学习建立了新的稳健基础

Conclusion: 通过解决训练不稳定和过拟合问题，提出的方法为3D策略学习提供了稳定可扩展的解决方案，实现了显著的性能提升

Abstract: 3D policy learning promises superior generalization and cross-embodiment transfer, but progress has been hindered by training instabilities and severe overfitting, precluding the adoption of powerful 3D perception models. In this work, we systematically diagnose these failures, identifying the omission of 3D data augmentation and the adverse effects of Batch Normalization as primary causes. We propose a new architecture coupling a scalable transformer-based 3D encoder with a diffusion decoder, engineered specifically for stability at scale and designed to leverage large-scale pre-training. Our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art 3D baselines on challenging manipulation benchmarks, establishing a new and robust foundation for scalable 3D imitation learning. Project Page: https://r3d-policy.github.io/

</details>


### [132] [GlobalSplat: Efficient Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting via Global Scene Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15284)
*Roni Itkin,Noam Issachar,Yehonatan Keypur,Yehonatan Keypur,Anpei Chen,Sagie Benaim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GlobalSplat提出了一种基于"先对齐后解码"原则的全局场景表示框架，通过紧凑的全局潜在表示编码多视角输入，解决了传统方法在表示紧凑性、重建速度和渲染质量之间的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D高斯泼溅方法中的空间分配策略存在根本性缺陷：基于迭代优化或前馈推理的方法都依赖于局部、启发式的分配策略，缺乏全局场景感知能力。特别是当前的前馈方法大多是像素对齐或体素对齐的，通过将像素反投影到密集的视角对齐基元中，将冗余性烘焙到3D资产中。随着输入视角增加，表示尺寸增大，全局一致性变得脆弱。

Method: 提出GlobalSplat框架，采用"先对齐后解码"原则：首先学习紧凑的全局潜在场景表示，编码多视角输入并解析跨视角对应关系，然后再解码为显式3D几何。采用从粗到细的训练课程，逐步增加解码容量，原生防止表示膨胀。不使用预训练的像素预测主干或重用密集基线的潜在特征。

Result: 在RealEstate10K和ACID数据集上，模型仅使用16K个高斯基元就能实现有竞争力的新视角合成性能，显著少于密集流水线所需数量，获得轻量的4MB存储占用。推理速度显著快于基线方法，单次前向传播在78毫秒内完成。

Conclusion: GlobalSplat通过全局潜在表示框架解决了3D高斯泼溅中的空间分配问题，实现了表示紧凑性、重建速度和渲染质量之间的更好平衡，为高效3D场景重建提供了新思路。

Abstract: The efficient spatial allocation of primitives serves as the foundation of 3D Gaussian Splatting, as it directly dictates the synergy between representation compactness, reconstruction speed, and rendering fidelity. Previous solutions, whether based on iterative optimization or feed-forward inference, suffer from significant trade-offs between these goals, mainly due to the reliance on local, heuristic-driven allocation strategies that lack global scene awareness. Specifically, current feed-forward methods are largely pixel-aligned or voxel-aligned. By unprojecting pixels into dense, view-aligned primitives, they bake redundancy into the 3D asset. As more input views are added, the representation size increases and global consistency becomes fragile. To this end, we introduce GlobalSplat, a framework built on the principle of align first, decode later. Our approach learns a compact, global, latent scene representation that encodes multi-view input and resolves cross-view correspondences before decoding any explicit 3D geometry. Crucially, this formulation enables compact, globally consistent reconstructions without relying on pretrained pixel-prediction backbones or reusing latent features from dense baselines. Utilizing a coarse-to-fine training curriculum that gradually increases decoded capacity, GlobalSplat natively prevents representation bloat. On RealEstate10K and ACID, our model achieves competitive novel-view synthesis performance while utilizing as few as 16K Gaussians, significantly less than required by dense pipelines, obtaining a light 4MB footprint. Further, GlobalSplat enables significantly faster inference than the baselines, operating under 78 milliseconds in a single forward pass. Project page is available at https://r-itk.github.io/globalsplat/

</details>


### [133] [AnimationBench: Are Video Models Good at Character-Centric Animation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15299)
*Leyi Wu,Pengjun Fang,Kai Sun,Yazhou Xing,Yinwei Wu,Songsong Wang,Ziqi Huang,Dan Zhou,Yingqing He,Ying-Cong Chen,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了AnimationBench，这是首个专门用于评估动画风格图像到视频生成任务的系统性基准测试，解决了现有基准测试在评估动画风格视频时存在的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成基准测试主要针对写实风格视频设计，难以有效评估动画风格视频的生成质量。动画视频具有风格化外观、夸张动作和角色一致性等特点，而现有基准测试使用固定提示词集和刚性评估流程，缺乏对开放域内容和自定义评估需求的灵活性。

Method: AnimationBench将动画十二原则和IP保护原则转化为可测量的评估维度，同时包含更广泛的质量维度（语义一致性、动作合理性、相机运动一致性等）。该基准支持标准化闭集评估以实现可重复比较，以及灵活的开放集评估用于诊断分析，并利用视觉语言模型进行可扩展的评估。

Result: 大量实验表明，AnimationBench与人类判断具有良好的一致性，能够揭示被写实导向基准测试忽视的动画特定质量差异，从而对最先进的I2V模型提供更具信息量和区分度的评估。

Conclusion: AnimationBench填补了动画风格图像到视频生成评估的空白，通过系统化的评估框架和灵活的评估模式，为动画视频生成领域提供了更准确、更全面的评估工具。

Abstract: Video generation has advanced rapidly, with recent methods producing increasingly convincing animated results. However, existing benchmarks-largely designed for realistic videos-struggle to evaluate animation-style generation with its stylized appearance, exaggerated motion, and character-centric consistency. Moreover, they also rely on fixed prompt sets and rigid pipelines, offering limited flexibility for open-domain content and custom evaluation needs. To address this gap, we introduce AnimationBench, the first systematic benchmark for evaluating animation image-to-video generation. AnimationBench operationalizes the Twelve Basic Principles of Animation and IP Preservation into measurable evaluation dimensions, together with Broader Quality Dimensions including semantic consistency, motion rationality, and camera motion consistency. The benchmark supports both a standardized close-set evaluation for reproducible comparison and a flexible open-set evaluation for diagnostic analysis, and leverages visual-language models for scalable assessment. Extensive experiments show that AnimationBench aligns well with human judgment and exposes animation-specific quality differences overlooked by realism-oriented benchmarks, leading to more informative and discriminative evaluation of state-of-the-art I2V models.

</details>


### [134] [Think in Latent Thoughts: A New Paradigm for Gloss-Free Sign Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15301)
*Yiyang Jiang,Li Zhang,Xiao-Yong Wei,Li Qing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于推理的手语翻译框架，使用有序潜在思维序列作为视频与生成文本之间的中间层，通过"先计划后落地"的解码方法提升翻译的连贯性和忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手语翻译系统通常假设简短的手语片段直接对应口语单词，但这种假设在实际中会失效，因为手语者经常利用上下文、空间和动作动态创造意义。作者认为手语翻译主要是跨模态推理任务，而非简单的视频到文本转换。

Method: 1. 引入推理驱动的手语翻译框架，使用有序潜在思维序列作为视频与生成文本之间的显式中间层；2. 采用"先计划后落地"的解码方法：模型先决定要说什么，然后回看视频寻找证据；3. 构建并发布了新的大规模无注释手语翻译数据集，具有更强的上下文依赖性和更现实的意义。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，该方法相比现有的无注释方法取得了持续的性能提升。

Conclusion: 手语翻译应被视为跨模态推理任务而非简单转换，通过引入潜在思维中间层和计划-落地解码策略，可以显著提升翻译的连贯性和忠实度，该方法在多个基准上优于现有方法。

Abstract: Many SLT systems quietly assume that brief chunks of signing map directly to spoken-language words. That assumption breaks down because signers often create meaning on the fly using context, space, and movement. We revisit SLT and argue that it is mainly a cross-modal reasoning task, not just a straightforward video-to-text conversion. We thus introduce a reasoning-driven SLT framework that uses an ordered sequence of latent thoughts as an explicit middle layer between the video and the generated text. These latent thoughts gradually extract and organize meaning over time. On top of this, we use a plan-then-ground decoding method: the model first decides what it wants to say, and then looks back at the video to find the evidence. This separation improves coherence and faithfulness. We also built and released a new large-scale gloss-free SLT dataset with stronger context dependencies and more realistic meanings. Experiments across several benchmarks show consistent gains over existing gloss-free methods. Code and data will be released upon acceptance at https://github.com/fletcherjiang/SignThought.

</details>


### [135] [RAD-2: Scaling Reinforcement Learning in a Generator-Discriminator Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15308)
*Hao Gao,Shaoyu Chen,Yifan Zhu,Yuehao Song,Wenyu Liu,Qian Zhang,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RAD-2：一种用于闭环规划的生成器-判别器统一框架，通过扩散模型生成多样化轨迹候选，RL优化的判别器根据长期驾驶质量重新排序，显著降低碰撞率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散模型的规划器虽然能有效建模复杂轨迹分布，但存在随机不稳定性和缺乏纠正性负反馈的问题，特别是在纯模仿学习训练时。需要开发既能建模多模态未来不确定性，又能在闭环交互中保持鲁棒性的运动规划器。

Method: 提出RAD-2统一框架：1）扩散模型生成器产生多样化轨迹候选；2）RL优化判别器根据长期驾驶质量重新排序候选轨迹；3）引入时间一致性组相对策略优化（TC-GRPO）利用时间相干性缓解信用分配问题；4）提出在线生成器优化（OGO）将闭环反馈转化为结构化纵向优化信号；5）开发BEV-Warp高吞吐量仿真环境，通过空间扭曲直接在鸟瞰图特征空间进行闭环评估。

Result: RAD-2相比强基于扩散的规划器减少56%的碰撞率。真实世界部署进一步展示了在复杂城市交通中感知安全性和驾驶平滑度的提升。

Conclusion: RAD-2通过生成器-判别器解耦设计有效解决了扩散规划器的随机不稳定性和缺乏负反馈问题，结合TC-GRPO和OGO优化策略以及高效仿真环境，实现了在保持轨迹多样性的同时显著提升闭环规划性能。

Abstract: High-level autonomous driving requires motion planners capable of modeling multimodal future uncertainties while remaining robust in closed-loop interactions. Although diffusion-based planners are effective at modeling complex trajectory distributions, they often suffer from stochastic instabilities and the lack of corrective negative feedback when trained purely with imitation learning. To address these issues, we propose RAD-2, a unified generator-discriminator framework for closed-loop planning. Specifically, a diffusion-based generator is used to produce diverse trajectory candidates, while an RL-optimized discriminator reranks these candidates according to their long-term driving quality. This decoupled design avoids directly applying sparse scalar rewards to the full high-dimensional trajectory space, thereby improving optimization stability. To further enhance reinforcement learning, we introduce Temporally Consistent Group Relative Policy Optimization, which exploits temporal coherence to alleviate the credit assignment problem. In addition, we propose On-policy Generator Optimization, which converts closed-loop feedback into structured longitudinal optimization signals and progressively shifts the generator toward high-reward trajectory manifolds. To support efficient large-scale training, we introduce BEV-Warp, a high-throughput simulation environment that performs closed-loop evaluation directly in Bird's-Eye View feature space via spatial warping. RAD-2 reduces the collision rate by 56% compared with strong diffusion-based planners. Real-world deployment further demonstrates improved perceived safety and driving smoothness in complex urban traffic.

</details>


### [136] [TokenLight: Precise Lighting Control in Images using Attribute Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15310)
*Sumit Chaturvedi,Yannick Hold-Geoffroy,Mengwei Ren,Jingyuan Liu,He Zhang,Yiqun Mei,Julie Dorsey,Zhixin Shu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TokenLight方法，通过属性令牌编码多种光照属性，实现照片中光照的精确连续控制，无需显式逆渲染监督即可理解光照与场景几何、遮挡和材质的交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像重光照方法通常难以实现对多种光照属性（如强度、颜色、环境光照、漫反射水平和3D光源位置）的精确连续控制，特别是在复杂场景中处理光照与几何、遮挡和材质交互时存在挑战。

Method: 将重光照建模为条件图像生成任务，引入属性令牌编码不同的光照因子；在大规模合成数据集（带有真实光照标注）上训练，并补充少量真实捕获数据以提高真实感和泛化能力。

Result: 在合成和真实图像的各种重光照任务中（包括控制场景内照明灯具和使用虚拟光源编辑环境光照）均达到最先进的定量和定性性能；模型展现出对光照与场景几何、遮挡和材质交互的内在理解能力。

Conclusion: TokenLight方法通过属性令牌实现了对图像中多种光照属性的精确连续控制，无需显式逆渲染监督即可生成逼真的光照效果，即使在传统挑战性场景（如将光源置于物体内部或重光照透明材质）中也能产生合理结果。

Abstract: This paper presents a method for image relighting that enables precise and continuous control over multiple illumination attributes in a photograph. We formulate relighting as a conditional image generation task and introduce attribute tokens to encode distinct lighting factors such as intensity, color, ambient illumination, diffuse level, and 3D light positions. The model is trained on a large-scale synthetic dataset with ground-truth lighting annotations, supplemented by a small set of real captures to enhance realism and generalization. We validate our approach across a variety of relighting tasks, including controlling in-scene lighting fixtures and editing environment illumination using virtual light sources, on synthetic and real images. Our method achieves state-of-the-art quantitative and qualitative performance compared to prior work. Remarkably, without explicit inverse rendering supervision, the model exhibits an inherent understanding of how light interacts with scene geometry, occlusion, and materials, yielding convincing lighting effects even in traditionally challenging scenarios such as placing lights within objects or relighting transparent materials plausibly. Project page: vrroom.github.io/tokenlight/

</details>


### [137] [LeapAlign: Post-Training Flow Matching Models at Any Generation Step by Building Two-Step Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15311)
*Zhanhao Liang,Tao Yang,Jie Wu,Chengjian Feng,Liang Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LeapAlign是一种用于流匹配模型与人类偏好对齐的微调方法，通过设计两段跳跃缩短长轨迹，降低计算成本并实现从奖励到早期生成步骤的直接梯度传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有直接梯度方法在流匹配模型微调中存在两个主要问题：1）通过长轨迹反向传播导致内存成本过高和梯度爆炸；2）难以更新早期生成步骤，而这些步骤对最终图像的全局结构至关重要。

Method: LeapAlign通过设计两个连续跳跃来缩短长轨迹，每个跳跃跳过多个ODE采样步骤，在单步中预测未来潜在变量。通过随机化跳跃的起始和结束时间步，实现任意生成步骤的高效稳定更新。采用加权训练策略，为与长生成路径更一致的轨迹分配更高权重，并通过减少大梯度项权重而非完全移除来增强梯度稳定性。

Result: 在微调Flux模型时，LeapAlign在各项指标上均优于最先进的基于GRPO的方法和直接梯度方法，实现了更优的图像质量和图文对齐效果。

Conclusion: LeapAlign通过缩短轨迹和优化梯度传播机制，有效解决了流匹配模型微调中的计算效率和梯度稳定性问题，为人类偏好对齐提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the alignment of flow matching models with human preferences. A promising way is fine-tuning by directly backpropagating reward gradients through the differentiable generation process of flow matching. However, backpropagating through long trajectories results in prohibitive memory costs and gradient explosion. Therefore, direct-gradient methods struggle to update early generation steps, which are crucial for determining the global structure of the final image. To address this issue, we introduce LeapAlign, a fine-tuning method that reduces computational cost and enables direct gradient propagation from reward to early generation steps. Specifically, we shorten the long trajectory into only two steps by designing two consecutive leaps, each skipping multiple ODE sampling steps and predicting future latents in a single step. By randomizing the start and end timesteps of the leaps, LeapAlign leads to efficient and stable model updates at any generation step. To better use such shortened trajectories, we assign higher training weights to those that are more consistent with the long generation path. To further enhance gradient stability, we reduce the weights of gradient terms with large magnitude, instead of completely removing them as done in previous works. When fine-tuning the Flux model, LeapAlign consistently outperforms state-of-the-art GRPO-based and direct-gradient methods across various metrics, achieving superior image quality and image-text alignment.

</details>


### [138] [Bidirectional Cross-Modal Prompting for Event-Frame Asymmetric Stereo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15312)
*Ninghui Xu,Fabio Tosi,Lihui Wang,Jiawei Han,Luca Bartolomei,Zhiting Yao,Matteo Poggi,Stefano Mattoccia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Bi-CMPStereo框架，通过双向跨模态提示机制，充分利用事件相机和帧相机的互补特性，实现鲁棒的跨模态立体匹配，显著提升在快速运动和挑战性光照条件下的3D感知性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统帧相机在动态场景中存在时间分辨率有限和运动模糊的问题，而事件相机具有高动态范围和不受这些限制的优势。两种模态的互补特性使事件-帧非对称立体视觉在快速运动和挑战性光照条件下具有可靠的3D感知潜力，但模态差异常导致跨模态立体匹配中忽略特定领域的线索。

Method: 提出Bi-CMPStereo框架，采用双向跨模态提示机制：1）在目标规范空间内学习精细对齐的立体表示；2）通过将每个模态投影到事件和帧两个域中来整合互补表示，充分利用两个领域的语义和结构特征。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在准确性和泛化能力方面显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: Bi-CMPStereo框架通过双向跨模态提示有效解决了事件-帧立体匹配中的模态差异问题，实现了更鲁棒的3D感知，在快速运动和挑战性光照条件下表现出色。

Abstract: Conventional frame-based cameras capture rich contextual information but suffer from limited temporal resolution and motion blur in dynamic scenes. Event cameras offer an alternative visual representation with higher dynamic range free from such limitations. The complementary characteristics of the two modalities make event-frame asymmetric stereo promising for reliable 3D perception under fast motion and challenging illumination. However, the modality gap often leads to marginalization of domain-specific cues essential for cross-modal stereo matching. In this paper, we introduce Bi-CMPStereo, a novel bidirectional cross-modal prompting framework that fully exploits semantic and structural features from both domains for robust matching. Our approach learns finely aligned stereo representations within a target canonical space and integrates complementary representations by projecting each modality into both event and frame domains. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in accuracy and generalization.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [Anomaly Detection in IEC-61850 GOOSE Networks: Evaluating Unsupervised and Temporal Learning for Real-Time Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14233)
*Joseph Moore*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 评估无监督时序模型在IEC-61850 GOOSE协议入侵检测中的有效性，比较五种模型在实时性约束下的性能表现


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IEC-61850 GOOSE协议缺乏原生安全机制，易受重放、伪装和数据注入攻击，且面临严格延迟约束（4ms内）和标记攻击数据稀缺的挑战

Method: 在ERENO IEC-61850数据集上比较五种模型：监督式随机森林基线、前馈自编码器、三种循环序列自编码器（RNN、LSTM、GRU），并进行跨环境评估

Result: 监督式随机森林检测性能最佳（F1=0.9516）但无法满足实时约束（21.8ms）；所有无监督模型均满足4ms要求，其中GRU在准确性与延迟间取得最佳平衡（F1=0.8737 at 1.118ms）；跨环境评估显示所有模型在分布偏移下性能下降，但循环模型相对性能保持更好

Conclusion: 无监督时序模型是GOOSE入侵检测的实用选择，特别适用于标记训练数据不可用或需要大规模跨变电站部署的环境，其时序建模比拟合标记攻击分布具有更好的泛化能力

Abstract: The IEC-61850 GOOSE protocol underpins time-critical communication in modern digital substations but lacks native security mechanisms, leaving it vulnerable to replay, masquerade, and data injection attacks. Intrusion detection in this setting is challenging due to strict latency constraints (sub-4ms) and limited availability of labeled attack data. This paper evaluates whether unsupervised temporal modeling can provide effective and deployable anomaly detection for GOOSE networks. Five models are compared on the ERENO IEC-61850 dataset: a supervised Random Forest baseline, a feedforward Autoencoder, and three recurrent sequence autoencoders (RNN, LSTM, and GRU). The supervised Random Forest achieves the highest detection performance (F1=0.9516) but fails to meet real-time constraints at 21.8ms per prediction. All four unsupervised models satisfy the 4ms requirement, with the GRU achieving the best accuracy to latency tradeoff among them (F1=0.8737 at 1.118ms). A cross-environment evaluation on an independent dataset shows that all models degrade under distribution shift. However, recurrent models retain substantially higher relative performance than the supervised baseline, suggesting that temporal sequence modeling generalizes better than fitting labeled attack distributions. Anomaly thresholds for the unsupervised models are selected on a held out validation partition to avoid test set leakage. These results support unsupervised temporal models as a practical choice for real-time GOOSE intrusion detection, particularly in environments where labeled training data may be unavailable or where large-scale deployment across diverse substations is required.

</details>


### [140] [Sovereign 2.0: Control-Plane Sovereignty for Cloud Systems Under Disruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14242)
*Justin Stark,Scott Wilkie*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出Sovereign 2.0模型，将云主权从单纯的数据驻留和基础设施位置扩展到可执行的治理、运营和恢复控制，强调主权是证据支持的控制系统而非位置属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于数据驻留和基础设施位置的主权定义已不足够，在地缘政治动荡、法律风险和服务边界扩展的背景下，需要重新定义主权为对数字服务治理、运营和恢复的可执行控制能力。

Method: 提出Sovereign 2.0控制平面中心模型，包含三层风险保证框架（治理、运营、技术控制），强调后量子密码控制（TLS和密钥托管）作为长期主权信任的基础。

Result: 将主权重新定义为证据支持的控制系统，使主权结果能够在稳态和危机条件下被指定和持续证明，为云架构、采购和韧性设计提供新框架。

Conclusion: 主权应从位置属性转变为可执行的控制系统，通过Sovereign 2.0模型和风险保证框架实现跨联邦环境的治理权威、访问控制、密码信任和数据生命周期管理。

Abstract: Cloud sovereignty can no longer be defined by data residency or infrastructure location alone. Under conditions of geopolitical disruption, legal exposure, and expanding service boundaries, sovereignty must be understood as enforceable control over how digital services are governed, operated, and recovered.
  This paper introduces Sovereign 2.0, a control-plane-centric model that extends sovereignty beyond localisation to include governance authority, privileged access, cryptographic trust, data lifecycle control, observability, and incident response across federated environments. We define management sovereignty as the sovereign ability to govern, operate, evidence, and recover services regardless of underlying infrastructure dependencies.
  To operationalise this model, we propose a three-layer risk-assurance framework spanning governance, operational, and technical controls, enabling sovereign outcomes to be specified and continuously evidenced under both steady-state and crisis conditions. We further position post-quantum-ready cryptographic control, particularly TLS and key custody, as foundational to long-term sovereign trust.
  These contributions reframe sovereignty as an evidence-backed control system rather than a property of location, with implications for cloud architecture, procurement, and resilience design.

</details>


### [141] [Head Count: Privacy-Preserving Face-Based Crowd Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14250)
*Fatemeh Marzani,Thijs van Ede,Geert Heijenk,Maarten van Steen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 基于人脸识别的隐私保护人群计数系统，使用模糊提取器生成匿名标识符，结合同态加密的布隆过滤器实现跨时空计数而不泄露身份信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于携带设备的人群计数方法因隐私保护改进而失效，需要一种既能跨时空计数又能保护个人隐私的新方法。现有技术要么无法跨时空计数，要么会泄露个人身份信息。

Method: 提出一个隐私保护的人脸识别计数管道：1) 摄像头检测人脸并提取特征；2) 使用模糊提取器生成匿名标识符；3) 删除原始人脸图像；4) 将标识符插入同态加密的布隆过滤器；5) 在加密数据上直接进行无感知的集合成员测试。

Result: 初步评估显示该方法具有良好前景，能够实现跨时空的人群计数，同时保护个人隐私，避免身份泄露。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了隐私保护与跨时空人群计数的矛盾，通过结合模糊提取器和同态加密技术，实现了既能准确计数又能保护个人身份信息的安全系统。

Abstract: An important aspect of crowd monitoring is knowing how many people we are dealing with. Sometimes, knowing the size of a crowd in a single location and at a specific moment is enough. Matters become problematic when counting the same people across dif ferent locations or counting them over longer periods of time. In those cases, we need to identify and later reidentify a person, which immediately leads to privacy concerns. Until recently, solutions have been based on unique identification of carry-on devices, yet privacy improvements have caused transmitted information to be randomized, rendering this technique mostly useless. We propose to use biometric data instead. We introduce a pipeline that counts people based on face recognition, yet without ever being able to reveal the identity of individuals. To count, a camera initially detects a face, extracts its features, and derives an identifier using a fuzzy extractor. The original facial image is then deleted. Identifiers are inserted into homomorphically encrypted Bloom filters. This allows oblivious set membership testing directly on encrypted data, enabling the system to count across locations or across different moments, without revealing any identities. We provide an initial evaluation of our method that shows promising results.

</details>


### [142] [Challenges and Future Directions in Agentic Reverse Engineering Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14317)
*Salem Radey,Jack West,Kassem Fawaz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文分析了基于大语言模型的智能体系统在二进制逆向工程任务中的表现，识别了现有系统的局限性，并提出了未来改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于大语言模型的智能体系统在复杂安全任务（特别是二进制逆向工程）中应用日益广泛，但这些系统在现实场景中仍面临显著限制。当前最先进的系统在处理涉及混淆、时序和独特架构的复杂逆向工程场景时仍然失败。

Method: 通过分析现有智能体工具的使用情况，研究了静态、动态和混合智能体在逆向工程任务中的表现。识别了多个关键限制因素。

Result: 研究发现现有智能体系统存在几个主要局限性：令牌约束、处理混淆代码的困难、缺乏程序防护机制。这些限制导致系统在复杂逆向工程场景中表现不佳。

Conclusion: 论文从安全角度概述了当前挑战，并为系统设计者指明了未来发展方向，以克服基于大语言模型的智能体系统在二进制逆向工程中的现有局限性。

Abstract: Agentic systems built on large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being used for complex security tasks, including binary reverse engineering (RE). Despite recent growth in popularity and capability, these systems continue to face limitations in realistic settings. Cutting-edge systems still fail in complex RE scenarios that involve obfuscation, timing, and unique architecture. In this work, we examine how agentic systems perform reverse engineering tasks with static, dynamic, and hybrid agents. Through an analysis of existing agentic tool usage, we identify several limitations, including token constraints, struggles with obfuscation, and a lack of program guardrails. From these findings, we outline current challenges and position future directions for system designers to overcome from a security perspective.

</details>


### [143] [Understanding Student Experiences with TLS Client Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14330)
*Abubakar Sadiq Shittu,Clay Shubert,John Sadik,Scott Ruoti*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 对46名计算机科学学生进行的mTLS可用性纵向研究表明，虽然日常使用流畅，但初始设置是主要瓶颈，只有9%参与者完全理解证书认证的安全含义，表明mTLS用户体验与普通用户严重脱节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管mTLS（双向TLS）为客户端和服务器提供了强大的基于证书的身份验证，但在面向用户的网站中采用率仍然很低。研究旨在通过纵向研究了解mTLS在实际使用中的可用性问题，特别是针对非PKI专家的用户群体。

Method: 对46名计算机科学高年级本科生和研究生进行纵向研究，要求他们从零开始配置客户端证书，在一个学期的课程中用于日常身份验证，并在多个设备间管理凭证。研究采用现实部署环境，使用OpenSSL、自定义CA和3072位最小密钥要求。

Result: 初始设置是主要瓶颈；日常使用虽然流畅，但并未改善长期可用性感知。最令人担忧的是，只有9%的参与者完全理解基于证书认证的安全含义。即使对于技术背景强的学生，在现实部署环境中也面临显著困难。

Conclusion: mTLS用户体验与普通用户严重脱节，难以看到主流采用的前景，除非进行重大的平台级改变。研究提供了经验证据，表明即使对技术用户，mTLS的当前实现也存在根本性的可用性问题。

Abstract: Mutual TLS (mTLS) provides strong, certificate-based authentication for both clients and servers, yet its adoption for user-facing websites remains rare. This paper presents a longitudinal study of mTLS usability, tracking 46 senior and graduate computer science students who configured client certificates from scratch, used them for routine authentication over a semester-long course, and managed credentials across multiple devices. The results reveal that initial setup is a major bottleneck; while daily use was considered smooth, it did not improve long-term usability perceptions. Most concerningly, only 9% of participants fully understood the security implications of certificate-based authentication. We conclude that in a realistic, tooling-heavy deployment utilizing OpenSSL, a custom CA, and a 3072-bit minimum key requirement, even highly technical students struggled significantly. We argue this provides empirical evidence that today mTLS user experience is fundamentally misaligned with non-PKI specialists, and it is difficult to see a path toward mainstream adoption without substantial platform-level changes.

</details>


### [144] [EdgeDetect: Importance-Aware Gradient Compression with Homomorphic Aggregation for Federated Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14663)
*Noor Islam S. Mohammad*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: EdgeDetect：面向6G-IoT环境的通信高效、隐私感知的联邦入侵检测系统，通过梯度智能化和同态加密实现32倍通信压缩，同时保持高检测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习在入侵检测中存在高通信开销（全精度梯度传输）和隐私风险（梯度推断攻击），难以适应带宽受限的6G-IoT边缘环境。

Method: 提出梯度智能化（基于中位数的统计二值化），将本地更新压缩为{+1,-1}表示；结合Paillier同态加密保护二值化梯度，防止诚实但好奇的服务器攻击。

Result: 在CIC-IDS2017数据集上达到98.0%多类准确率和97.9%宏F1分数，匹配集中式基线；每轮通信从450MB降至14MB（减少96.9%）；树莓派部署显示4.2MB内存、0.8ms延迟、12mJ/推理；在5%投毒攻击和严重不平衡下保持87%准确率和0.95少数类F1。

Conclusion: EdgeDetect为下一代边缘入侵检测建立了实用的准确性、通信和隐私权衡，适用于资源受限的6G-IoT环境。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative intrusion detection without raw data exchange, but conventional FL incurs high communication overhead from full-precision gradient transmission and remains vulnerable to gradient inference attacks. This paper presents EdgeDetect, a communication-efficient and privacy-aware federated IDS for bandwidth-constrained 6G-IoT environments. EdgeDetect introduces gradient smartification, a median-based statistical binarization that compresses local updates to $\{+1,-1\}$ representations, reducing uplink payload by $32\times$ while preserving convergence. We further integrate Paillier homomorphic encryption over binarized gradients, protecting against honest-but-curious servers without exposing individual updates. Experiments on CIC-IDS2017 (2.8M flows, 7 attack classes) demonstrate $98.0\%$ multi-class accuracy and $97.9\%$ macro F1-score, matching centralized baselines, while reducing per-round communication from $450$~MB to $14$~MB ($96.9\%$ reduction). Raspberry Pi-4 deployment confirms edge feasibility: $4.2$~MB memory, $0.8$~ms latency, and $12$~mJ per inference with $<0.5\%$ accuracy loss. Under $5\%$ poisoning attacks and severe imbalance, EdgeDetect maintains $87\%$ accuracy and $0.95$ minority class F1 ($p<0.001$), establishing a practical accuracy, communication, and privacy tradeoff for next-generation edge intrusion detection.

</details>


### [145] [Beyond Nodes vs. Edges: A Multi-View Fusion Framework for Provenance-Based Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14685)
*Fan Yang,Binyan Xu,Di Tang,Kehuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PROVFUSION：一个多视图检测框架，通过融合属性、结构和因果三个视图的异常信号，解决了现有基于溯源图的入侵检测方法在节点中心与边中心分析之间的粒度限制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于溯源图的入侵检测方法存在固有的粒度限制：节点中心检测器可能将良性行为变化误判为可疑，而边中心检测器可能因缺乏足够的上下文感知而漏检。节点中心与边中心分析之间存在分析偏差的持续差距。

Method: 提出PROVFUSION多视图检测框架，集成属性、结构和因果三个不同视图的异常信号。通过轻量级融合方案融合异构异常信号，并通过基于投票的集成过程确定最终异常决策，提供更一致和上下文感知的系统行为评估。

Result: 在九个广泛使用的基准数据集上的实验表明，PROVFUSION相比单一节点中心和边中心基线方法，实现了更高的检测准确率和更低的误报率，在不同场景下保持稳定的性能。

Conclusion: 多视图异常融合与基于投票的决策聚合为推进基于溯源的入侵检测提供了一个实用且有效的方向，能够在一致的分析流程中同时捕获实体级偏差和交互级异常。

Abstract: Provenance-based intrusion detection has emerged as a promising approach for analyzing complex attack behaviors through system-level provenance graphs. However, existing defense methods face an inherent granularity limitation. Node-centric detectors, which evaluate anomalies using entities' attributes and local structural patterns, may misclassify benign behavioral changes or configuration modifications as suspicious. In contrast, edge-centric detectors, which focus more on interactions, may lack sufficient contextual awareness of the involved entities, leading to missed detections when compromised entities perform seemingly ordinary operations. These analytical biases highlight a persistent gap between node-centric and edge-centric analyses. To mitigate this gap, we present PROVFUSION, a multi-view detection framework that integrates anomaly signals from three distinct views (i.e., attribute, structure, and causality). The framework fuses heterogeneous anomaly signals through lightweight fusion schemes and determines the final anomaly decisions through a voting-based integration process, providing a more consistent and context-aware assessment of system behavior. This design enables PROVFUSION to capture both entity level deviations and interaction-level anomalies within a consistent analytic pipeline. Experiments on nine widely used benchmark datasets demonstrate that PROVFUSION achieves higher detection accuracy and lower false-positive rates than single node- and edge-centric baselines, maintaining stable performance across scenarios. Overall, the results suggest that our multi-view anomaly fusion together with voting-based decision aggregation offers a practical and effective direction for advancing provenance-based intrusion detection.

</details>


### [146] [Efficient Fuzzy Private Set Intersection from Secret-shared OPRF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14909)
*Xinpeng Yang,Meng Hao,Chenkai Weng,Robert H. Deng,Yonggang Wen,Tianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出高效的模糊私有集合交集协议，针对Lp距离度量，主要利用对称密钥操作，实现线性复杂度，相比现有工作显著提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模糊PSI方案要么依赖昂贵的同态加密，要么复杂度不理想（如指数级维度依赖），导致实际效率低下，需要更高效的解决方案

Method: 基于对称密钥操作构建协议，核心组件是带秘密共享输出的可编程PRF，并采用前缀技术将对距离阈值δ的依赖从线性降低到对数级

Result: 协议实现线性通信和计算复杂度（与集合大小m,n、维度d、距离阈值δ成线性关系），实验显示相比现有工作运行时间提升9-145倍，通信成本降低3-19倍

Conclusion: 提出的模糊PSI协议在Lp距离度量下实现了显著性能提升，核心构建块可编程PRF具有独立研究价值，前缀技术特别适合大距离阈值场景

Abstract: Private set intersection (PSI) enables a sender holding a set $Q$ of size $m$ and a receiver holding a set $W$ of size $n$ to securely compute the intersection $Q \cap W$. Fuzzy PSI (FPSI) is a PSI variant where the receiver learns the items $q \in Q$ for which there exists some $w \in W$ satisfying $\mathsf{dist}(q, w) \le δ$ under a given distance metric. Although several FPSI works are proposed for $L_{p}$ distance metrics with $p \in [1, \infty]$, they either heavily rely on expensive homomorphic encryptions, or incur undesirable complexity, e.g., exponential to the element dimension, both of which lead to poor practical efficiency.
  In this work, we propose efficient FPSI protocols for $L_{p \in [1, \infty]}$ distance metrics, primarily leveraging significantly cheaper symmetric-key operations. Our protocols achieve linear communication and computation complexity in the set sizes $m,n$, the dimension $d$, and the distance threshold $δ$. Our core building block is an oblivious programmable PRF with secret-shared outputs, which may be of independent interest. Furthermore, we incorporate a prefix technique that reduces the dependence on the distance threshold $δ$ to logarithmic, which is particularly suitable for large $δ$.
  We implement our FPSI protocols and compare them with state-of-the-art constructions. Experimental results demonstrate that our protocols consistently and significantly outperform existing works across all settings. Specifically, our protocols achieve a speedup of $12{\sim}145\times$ in running time and a reduction of $3{\sim}8\times$ in communication cost compared to Gao et al.~(ASIACRYPT'24) and a speedup of $9{\sim}80\times$ in running time and a reduction of $5{\sim}19\times$ in communication cost compared to Dang et al.~(CCS'25).

</details>


### [147] [Robustness of Vision Foundation Models to Common Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14973)
*Hongbin Liu,Zhengyuan Jiang,Cheng Hong,Neil Zhenqiang Gong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究首次系统评估了视觉基础模型对常见图像编辑操作（如JPEG压缩、亮度对比度调整）的鲁棒性，提出了三个鲁棒性指标及其数学性质，评估了六个工业级基础模型，发现普遍不鲁棒，并提出了一种微调方法来提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型输出的图像嵌入向量会受到常见编辑操作（JPEG压缩、亮度对比度调整等）的影响，这些扰动可能改变嵌入向量并影响下游任务的性能。目前缺乏对基础模型在此类扰动下鲁棒性的系统性研究。

Method: 提出了三个鲁棒性评估指标，并形式化了五个期望的数学性质来分析这些指标。使用这些指标评估了六个工业级基础模型（OpenAI、Meta等）在九种常见扰动类别下的表现。还提出了一种微调方法来提升鲁棒性而不牺牲实用性。

Result: 发现所评估的基础模型普遍对常见扰动不鲁棒。常见扰动会降低下游应用性能（如分类准确率），且鲁棒性值可以预测性能影响。提出的微调方法能够有效提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 这是首个系统研究基础模型对常见图像扰动鲁棒性的工作，揭示了工业级基础模型在此类扰动下的脆弱性，提出的评估框架和微调方法为提升模型鲁棒性提供了实用方案。

Abstract: A vision foundation model outputs an embedding vector for an image, which can be affected by common editing operations (e.g., JPEG compression, brightness, contrast adjustments). These common perturbations alter embedding vectors and may impact the performance of downstream tasks using these embeddings. In this work, we present the first systematic study on foundation models' robustness to such perturbations. We propose three robustness metrics and formulate five desired mathematical properties for these metrics, analyzing which properties they satisfy or violate. Using these metrics, we evaluate six industry-scale foundation models (OpenAI, Meta) across nine common perturbation categories, finding them generally non-robust. We also show that common perturbations degrade downstream application performance (e.g., classification accuracy) and that robustness values can predict performance impacts. Finally, we propose a fine-tuning approach to improve robustness without sacrificing utility.

</details>


### [148] [Emulation-based System-on-Chip Security Verification: Challenges and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15073)
*Tanvir Rahman,Shuvagata Saha,Ahmed Y. Alhurubi,Sujan Kumar Saha,Farimah Farahmandi,Mark Tehranipoor*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文是关于基于仿真的安全验证与确认的综合调查与展望，探讨了硬件仿真在SoC安全验证中的关键作用、现有技术分类、工作流程、挑战及未来发展方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着SoC异构性增加、硬件/软件深度集成以及第三方IP的普及，安全验证已成为半导体设计的核心问题。传统仿真和形式验证难以暴露真实执行条件下、长时间软件驱动交互和对抗性刺激中出现的漏洞，因此需要更高吞吐量的硬件仿真技术来支持真实硬件/软件工作负载下的安全分析。

Method: 本文采用系统性调查方法，对基于仿真的安全验证领域进行结构化分析：1) 对现有工作进行分类整理，涵盖基于断言的安全检查、覆盖率驱动探索、对抗性测试、信息流追踪、故障注入和侧信道评估；2) 提供仿真使能的安全验证工作流程结构化视图，包括仪器化、激励生成、运行时监控和证据驱动分析；3) 分析实际挑战，如可观测性、可扩展性、属性规范和基于仿真的安全覆盖率指标定义。

Result: 通过系统化梳理，本文建立了基于仿真的安全验证的完整技术框架，明确了该领域的关键技术分类、工作流程组成和主要挑战。研究指出硬件仿真能够支持真实硬件/软件工作负载下的高吞吐量执行，同时保持足够保真度进行安全导向分析。

Conclusion: 硬件仿真为下一代流片前硬件安全保障提供了有前景的基础。未来发展方向包括AI辅助仿真、数字安全孪生、小芯片级安全探索、自动化漏洞评估和云规模安全仿真。本文为基于仿真的安全验证领域提供了系统性参考框架和前瞻性视角。

Abstract: Increasing system-on-chip (SoC) heterogeneity, deep hardware/software integration, and the proliferation of third-party intellectual property (IP) have brought security validation to the forefront of semiconductor design. While simulation and formal verification remain indispensable, they often struggle to expose vulnerabilities that emerge only under realistic execution conditions, long software-driven interactions, and adversarial stimuli. In this context, hardware emulation is emerging as an increasingly important pre-silicon verification technology because it enables higher-throughput execution of RTL designs under realistic hardware/software workloads while preserving sufficient fidelity for security-oriented analysis.
  This paper presents a comprehensive survey and perspective on emulation-based security verification and validation. We organize the landscape of prior work across assertion-based security checking, coverage-driven exploration, adversarial testing, information-flow tracking, fault injection, and side-channel-oriented evaluation. We provide a structured view of emulation-enabled security verification workflows, including instrumentation, stimulus generation, runtime monitoring, and evidence-driven analysis. We also examine practical challenges related to observability, scalability, property specification, and the definition of security-oriented coverage metrics for emulation-based verification. Finally, we discuss emerging directions such as AI-assisted emulation, digital security twins, chiplet-scale security exploration, automated vulnerability assessment, and cloud-scale secure emulation. Overall, this paper positions emulation as a promising foundation for the next generation of pre-silicon hardware security assurance.

</details>


### [149] [NFTDELTA: Detecting Permission Control Vulnerabilities in NFT Contracts through Multi-View Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15118)
*Hailu Kuang,Xiaoqi Li,Wenkai Li,Zongwei Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: NFTDELTA是一个基于静态分析和多视图学习的框架，用于检测NFT合约中的权限控制漏洞，通过序列特征和图特征的双视图分析，实现了高精度检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NFT合约中的权限控制漏洞可能导致重大财务损失，攻击者可利用这些弱点获取未授权访问或绕过关键权限检查。现有检测方法在全面性和准确性方面存在不足，需要更有效的检测框架来保护NFT生态系统。

Method: 提出NFTDELTA框架，采用静态分析提取函数控制流图信息，通过两个视图分析：序列特征（表示执行路径）和图特征（捕捉结构控制流）。将两个视图整合为统一代码表示，定义三类权限控制漏洞，通过多视图特征相似性分析的自定义检测器识别缺陷。

Result: 在795个流行NFT集合的评估中，识别出241个确认的权限控制漏洞，包括214个Bypass Auth Reentrancy、15个Weak Auth Validation和12个Loose Permission Management。手动验证显示检测器平均精度达97.92%，F1分数81.09%，证明其高可靠性和有效性。
