Conclusion: NFTDELTA框架通过多视图学习和静态分析，能够高效、准确地检测NFT合约中的权限控制漏洞，具有增强的效率和可扩展性，对保护NFT生态系统安全具有重要价值。

Abstract: Permission control vulnerabilities in Non-fungible token (NFT) contracts can result in significant financial losses, as attackers may exploit these weaknesses to gain unauthorized access or circumvent critical permission checks. In this paper, we propose NFTDELTA, a framework that leverages static analysis and multi-view learning to detect permission control vulnerabilities in NFT contracts. Specifically, we extract comprehensive function Control Flow Graph (CFG) information via two views: sequence features (representing execution paths) and graph features (capturing structural control flow). These two views are then integrated to create a unified code representation. We also define three specific categories of permission control vulnerabilities and employ a custom detector to identify defects through multi-view feature similarity analysis. Our evaluation of 795 popular NFT collections identified 241 confirmed permission control vulnerabilities, comprising 214 cases of Bypass Auth Reentrancy, 15 of Weak Auth Validation, and 12 of Loose Permission Management. Manual verification demonstrates the detector's high reliability, achieving an average precision of 97.92% and an F1-score of 81.09%. Furthermore, NFTDELTA demonstrates enhanced efficiency and scalability, proving its effectiveness in securing NFT ecosystems.

</details>


### [150] [Structural Dependency Analysis for Masked NTT Hardware: Scalable Pre-Silicon Verification of Post-Quantum Cryptographic Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15249)
*Ray Iskander,Khaled Kirah*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出四阶段验证层次结构，将一阶掩码验证扩展到生产级算术模块，应用于117万单元的ML-DSA/ML-KEM加速器，将手动审查从数百个结构标志减少到165个可操作候选


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后量子密码加速器需要侧信道抗性证据以获得FIPS 140-3认证，但现有精确掩码验证工具仅能处理几千个单元的小规模电路，无法应对生产级算术模块的验证需求

Method: 提出四阶段验证层次：D0/D1结构依赖分析、新鲜掩码细化、布尔单认证距离检查(SADC)和算术SADC。引入多周期扩展(MC-D1)重新分类模块状态。在ML-KEM Barrett约减模块上应用该流程

Result: 在117万单元的Adams Bridge ML-DSA/ML-KEM加速器上，结构分析在30个掩码子模块中秒级完成。MC-D1将12个模块从结构清洁重新分类为结构标记。在5,543单元的Barrett约减模块中，验证了198个结构标记线(54.5%)为一阶安全，报告165个为候选不安全线，0个不确定。所有结果与Z3和CVC5交叉验证一致

Conclusion: 该验证层次将手动审查从数百个结构标志减少到165个可操作候选，并提供数学证书，实现了生产级ML-KEM硬件的预硅侧信道证据生成

Abstract: Post-quantum cryptographic accelerators require side-channel resistance evidence for FIPS 140-3 certification. However, exact masking-verification tools scale only to gadgets of a few thousand cells. We present a four-stage verification hierarchy, D0/D1 structural dependency analysis, fresh-mask refinement, Boolean Single-Authentication Distance Checking (SADC), and arithmetic SADC, that extends sound first-order masking verification to production arithmetic modules. Applied to the 1.17-million-cell Adams Bridge ML-DSA/ML-KEM accelerator, structural analysis completes in seconds across all 30 masked submodules. A multi-cycle extension (MC-D1) reclassifies 12 modules from structurally clean to structurally flagged. On the 5,543-cell ML-KEM Barrett reduction module, the pipeline machine-verifies 198 of 363 structurally flagged wires (54.5%) as first-order secure, reports 165 as candidate insecure for designer triage (a sound upper bound), and leaves 0 indeterminate. Every verdict is cross validated by Z3 and CVC5 with 0 disagreements across 363 wires. The result narrows manual review from hundreds of structural flags to 165 actionable candidates with mathematical certificates, enabling pre-silicon side-channel evidence generation on production ML-KEM hardware.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [Doubly Outlier-Robust Online Infinite Hidden Markov Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14322)
*Horace Yiu,Leandro Sánchez-Betancourt,Álvaro Cartea,Gerardo Duran-Martin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对包含异常值和模型误设的流数据的鲁棒在线无限隐马尔可夫模型更新规则，通过后验影响函数定义鲁棒性，平衡适应性和鲁棒性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当流数据包含异常值且模型存在误设时，传统的在线无限隐马尔可夫模型缺乏鲁棒性，需要一种既能处理异常值又能保持模型适应性的方法

Method: 利用广义贝叶斯推断的最新进展，通过后验影响函数定义鲁棒性，提出批量鲁棒iHMM方法，引入两个可调参数平衡适应性和鲁棒性

Result: 在限价订单簿数据、小时电力需求和合成高维线性系统上，BR-iHMM相比竞争性在线贝叶斯方法将一步预测误差降低了高达67%

Conclusion: 该方法不仅提供了有界后验影响函数的理论保证，在实际应用中表现出优越的预测性能和可解释的在线学习能力

Abstract: We derive a robust update rule for the online infinite hidden Markov model (iHMM) for when the streaming data contains outliers and the model is misspecified. Leveraging recent advances in generalised Bayesian inference, we define robustness via the posterior influence function (PIF), and provide conditions under which the online iHMM has bounded PIF. Imposing robustness inevitably induces an adaptation lag for regime switching. Our method, which is called Batched Robust iHMM (BR-iHMM), balances adaptivity and robustness with two additional tunable parameters. Across limit order book data, hourly electricity demand, and a synthetic high-dimensional linear system, BR-iHMM reduces one-step-ahead forecasting error by up to 67% relative to competing online Bayesian methods. Together with theoretical guarantees of bounded PIF, our results highlight the practicality of our approach for both forecasting and interpretable online learning.

</details>


### [152] [Differentially Private Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14621)
*Jiamei Wu,Ce Zhang,Zhipeng Cai,Jingsen Kong,Bei Jiang,Linglong Kong,Lingchen Kong*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在差分隐私下进行保形预测的统计高效方法，包括非分割的差分保形预测和完全私有的DPCP方法，相比现有方法能在相同隐私预算下产生更紧的预测集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保形预测作为一种简单灵活的预测不确定性量化框架，在差分隐私下的部署面临统计效率损失问题。传统的数据分割方法会降低效率，需要开发既能保护隐私又能保持统计效率的方法。

Method: 首先提出差分保形预测，这是一种非分割的保形过程，避免了数据分割导致的效率损失。然后基于此开发了完全私有的DPCP方法，结合差分隐私模型训练和私有分位数机制进行校准。利用差分隐私机制的稳定性特性建立与oracle保形预测的直接联系。

Result: 建立了DPCP的端到端隐私保证，并在额外正则条件下研究了其覆盖特性。在经验风险最小化和一般回归模型下分析了差分保形预测和DPCP的效率，证明DPCP能在相同隐私预算下比现有私有分割保形方法产生更紧的预测集。在合成和真实数据集上的数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的差分保形预测和DPCP方法为差分隐私下的保形预测提供了统计高效的解决方案，避免了传统数据分割方法的效率损失，在保持隐私保护的同时提高了预测集的紧致性。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) has attracted broad attention as a simple and flexible framework for uncertainty quantification through prediction sets. In this work, we study how to deploy CP under differential privacy (DP) in a statistically efficient manner. We first introduce differential CP, a non-splitting conformal procedure that avoids the efficiency loss caused by data splitting and serves as a bridge between oracle CP and private conformal inference. By exploiting the stability properties of DP mechanisms, differential CP establishes a direct connection to oracle CP and inherits corresponding validity behavior. Building on this idea, we develop Differentially Private Conformal Prediction (DPCP), a fully private procedure that combines DP model training with a private quantile mechanism for calibration. We establish the end-to-end privacy guarantee of DPCP and investigate its coverage properties under additional regularity conditions. We further study the efficiency of both differential CP and DPCP under empirical risk minimization and general regression models, showing that DPCP can produce tighter prediction sets than existing private split conformal approaches under the same privacy budget. Numerical experiments on synthetic and real datasets demonstrate the practical effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [153] [Expert-Guided Class-Conditional Goodness-of-Fit Scores for Interpretable Classification with Informative Missingness: An Application to Seismic Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14809)
*Shahar Cohen,David M. Steinberg,Yael Radzyner,Yochai Ben Horin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一个处理信息缺失、整合专家先验知识、生成可解释决策规则的分类框架，应用于地震监测领域


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决分类问题中的三个关键挑战：普遍的信息缺失、将部分专家先验知识整合到学习过程中、以及需要可解释的决策规则

Method: 通过专家指导的类条件模型编码先验知识，构建少量可解释的拟合优度特征，量化观测数据与专家模型的一致性，结合透明辅助摘要构建简单判别分类器

Result: 该方法作为透明筛查工具具有强大潜力，能减少专家分析工作量；模拟实验显示，在小样本训练时，这种可解释的专家指导方法甚至能优于标准机器学习分类器

Conclusion: 提出的框架成功解决了信息缺失、专家知识整合和可解释性需求，在核试验禁约地震监测中表现出色，特别适合小样本场景

Abstract: We study a classification problem with three key challenges: pervasive informative missingness, the integration of partial prior expert knowledge into the learning process, and the need for interpretable decision rules. We propose a framework that encodes prior knowledge through an expert-guided class-conditional model for one or more classes, and use this model to construct a small set of interpretable goodness-of-fit features. The features quantify how well the observed data agree with the expert model, isolating the contributions of different aspects of the data, including both observed and missing components. These features are combined with a few transparent auxiliary summaries in a simple discriminative classifier, resulting in a decision rule that is easy to inspect and justify. We develop and apply the framework in the context of seismic monitoring used to assess compliance with the Comprehensive Nuclear-Test-Ban Treaty. We show that the method has strong potential as a transparent screening tool, reducing workload for expert analysts. A simulation designed to isolate the contribution of the proposed framework shows that this interpretable expert-guided method can even outperform strong standard machine-learning classifiers, particularly when training samples are small.

</details>


### [154] [Best of both worlds: Stochastic & adversarial best-arm identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14860)
*Yasin Abbasi-Yadkori,Peter L. Bartlett,Victor Gabillon,Alan Malek,Michal Valko*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 研究多臂老虎机最优臂识别问题，提出一个能在随机和对抗性奖励下都表现良好的参数无关算法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机均匀策略在对抗性奖励下能达到最优错误率，但在随机奖励下表现次优。需要设计一个既能适应随机奖励又能对抗对抗性奖励，且无需知道奖励性质的通用学习器。

Method: 首先证明设计完全通用的学习器是不可能的，然后给出在对抗性约束下随机问题的最优错误率下界，最后设计一个简单的参数无关算法，其错误概率在随机问题中匹配下界（对数因子内），同时对抗对抗性奖励。

Result: 证明了设计完全通用的学习器不可能；给出了对抗性约束下随机问题的最优错误率下界；设计的参数无关算法在随机问题中错误率匹配下界（对数因子内），同时保持对抗性鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 在随机和对抗性多臂老虎机最优臂识别问题中，存在性能权衡。虽然无法设计完全通用的最优学习器，但可以设计在对抗性约束下在随机问题子集上达到最优错误率的算法，提出的参数无关算法实现了这一目标。

Abstract: We study bandit best-arm identification with arbitrary and potentially adversarial rewards. A simple random uniform learner obtains the optimal rate of error in the adversarial scenario. However, this type of strategy is suboptimal when the rewards are sampled stochastically. Therefore, we ask: Can we design a learner that performs optimally in both the stochastic and adversarial problems while not being aware of the nature of the rewards? First, we show that designing such a learner is impossible in general. In particular, to be robust to adversarial rewards, we can only guarantee optimal rates of error on a subset of the stochastic problems. We give a lower bound that characterizes the optimal rate in stochastic problems if the strategy is constrained to be robust to adversarial rewards. Finally, we design a simple parameter-free algorithm and show that its probability of error matches (up to log factors) the lower bound in stochastic problems, and it is also robust to adversarial ones.

</details>


### [155] [MinShap: A Modified Shapley Value Approach for Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15107)
*Chenghui Zheng,Garvesh Raskutti*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出MinShap方法，通过修改Shapley值框架，使用最小边际贡献而非平均边际贡献来进行特征选择，解决高维非线性依赖特征选择问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统特征选择方法在处理非线性关系和依赖特征时面临挑战，而Shapley值虽然广泛用于特征归因，但不适合特征选择，因为它同时捕捉直接效应和通过其他特征的间接效应

Method: 提出MinShap方法，修改Shapley值框架，考虑特征在所有排列中的最小边际贡献而非平均边际贡献；基于DAG中的忠实性假设提供理论基础；引入基于多重检验/p值视角的相关算法套件

Result: MinShap在数值模拟和真实数据实验中，在准确性和稳定性方面优于LOCO、GCM和Lasso等最先进的特征选择算法；提供Type I错误保证；相关算法套件在低样本量设置下表现更好

Conclusion: MinShap成功将Shapley值框架适应于特征选择任务，通过最小边际贡献方法有效处理非线性依赖特征，提供理论保证并在实验中表现出优越性能

Abstract: Feature selection is a classical problem in statistics and machine learning, and it continues to remain an extremely challenging problem especially in the context of unknown non-linear relationships with dependent features. On the other hand, Shapley values are a classic solution concept from cooperative game theory that is widely used for feature attribution in general non-linear models with highly-dependent features. However, Shapley values are not naturally suited for feature selection since they tend to capture both direct effects from each feature to the response and indirect effects through other features. In this paper, we combine the advantages of Shapley values and adapt them to feature selection by proposing \emph{MinShap}, a modification of the Shapley value framework along with a suite of other related algorithms. In particular for MinShap, instead of taking the average marginal contributions over permutations of features, considers the minimum marginal contribution across permutations. We provide a theoretical foundation motivated by the faithfulness assumption in DAG (directed acyclic graphical models), a guarantee for the Type I error of MinShap, and show through numerical simulations and real data experiments that MinShap tends to outperform state-of-the-art feature selection algorithms such as LOCO, GCM and Lasso in terms of both accuracy and stability. We also introduce a suite of algorithms related to MinShap by using the multiple testing/p-value perspective that improves performance in lower-sample settings and provide supporting theoretical guarantees.

</details>


### [156] [Amortized Optimal Transport from Sliced Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15114)
*Minh-Phuc Truong,Khai Nguyen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出两种基于摊销优化的最优运输计划预测方法：回归型摊销OT（RA-OT）和目标型摊销OT（OA-OT），利用切片OT的Kantorovich势函数实现跨多个测度对的高效OT计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决重复最优运输问题时的计算效率问题，通过摊销优化方法利用先前实例学习的信息快速近似新解，同时避免对测度特定结构（如离散情况下的原子数量）的依赖。

Method: 提出两种摊销策略：1) RA-OT：将原始OT问题的Kantorovich势函数作为响应，切片OT获得的势函数作为预测变量，建立函数回归模型并通过最小二乘法估计；2) OA-OT：通过优化Kantorovich对偶目标估计函数模型参数。两种方法都从估计的势函数中恢复预测的OT计划。

Result: 方法在MNIST数字传输、颜色迁移、球面数据上的供需运输以及小批量OT条件流匹配等任务中表现出有效性，实现了高精度且对测度结构具有独立性。

Conclusion: 提出的RA-OT和OA-OT方法作为摊销OT技术，能够高效解决跨不同测度对的重复OT问题，通过利用切片OT提供的结构实现更简洁的模型，同时保持高准确性。

Abstract: We propose a novel amortized optimization method for predicting optimal transport (OT) plans across multiple pairs of measures by leveraging Kantorovich potentials derived from sliced OT. We introduce two amortization strategies: regression-based amortization (RA-OT) and objective-based amortization (OA-OT). In RA-OT, we formulate a functional regression model that treats Kantorovich potentials from the original OT problem as responses and those obtained from sliced OT as predictors, and estimate these models via least-squares methods. In OA-OT, we estimate the parameters of the functional model by optimizing the Kantorovich dual objective. In both approaches, the predicted OT plan is subsequently recovered from the estimated potentials. As amortized OT methods, both RA-OT and OA-OT enable efficient solutions to repeated OT problems across different measure pairs by reusing information learned from prior instances to rapidly approximate new solutions. Moreover, by exploiting the structure provided by sliced OT, the proposed models are more parsimonious, independent of specific structures of the measures, such as the number of atoms in the discrete case, while achieving high accuracy. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approaches on tasks including MNIST digit transport, color transfer, supply-demand transportation on spherical data, and mini-batch OT conditional flow matching.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [A note on kernel density estimators with optimal bandwidths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14181)
*Nils Lid Hjort,Stephen G. Walker*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 核密度估计器的最优带宽对应的累积分布函数以概率趋于1落在经验分布函数的任何置信区间之外


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究核密度估计器的最优带宽选择对累积分布函数估计的影响，特别是其与经验分布函数置信区间的关系

Method: 理论分析，研究核密度估计器在最优带宽下的累积分布函数性质，分析其与经验分布函数置信区间的渐近关系

Result: 当样本量增加时，核密度估计器最优带宽对应的累积分布函数以概率趋于1落在经验分布函数的任何置信区间之外

Conclusion: 核密度估计器的最优带宽选择导致其累积分布函数估计与经验分布函数存在系统性偏差，无法通过经验分布函数的置信区间来评估其准确性

Abstract: We show that the cumulative distribution function corresponding to a kernel density estimator with optimal bandwidth lies outside any confidence interval, around the empirical distribution function, with probability tending to 1 as the sample size increases.

</details>


### [158] [Early-stopped aggregation: Adaptive inference with computational efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14404)
*Ilsang Ohn,Shitao Fan,Jungbin Jun,Lizhen Lin*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出早期停止聚合(ESA)框架，通过仅计算少量简单模型而非全部候选模型来提高计算效率，适用于贝叶斯和频率主义推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型选择或聚合方法需要计算所有候选模型的估计量，即使真实数据生成过程相对简单，这导致计算效率低下。缺乏先验知识时，需要计算过多不必要的复杂模型。

Method: 提出早期停止聚合(ESA)框架：使用早期停止准则仅计算少量简单模型，然后仅聚合这些模型进行最终推断。该方法适用于变分贝叶斯模型选择、频率主义估计（包括惩罚估计）等多种学习范式。

Result: ESA在变分贝叶斯设置下实现了最优自适应收缩率，扩展到变分经验贝叶斯，并在频率主义聚合中建立了相应理论。研究揭示了早期停止贝叶斯与频率主义惩罚聚合之间的统一性，两者共享包含数据拟合项和复杂度控制项的"能量"泛函。

Conclusion: ESA框架提供了一种高效且通用的模型聚合方法，显著提高了计算效率，同时保持了统计最优性。该方法在贝叶斯和频率主义范式下都表现出色，为自适应统计推断提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: When considering a model selection or, more generally, an aggregation approach for adaptive statistical inference, it is often necessary to compute estimators over a wide range of model complexities including unnecessarily large models even when the true data-generating process is relatively simple, due to the lack of prior knowledge. This requirement can lead to substantial computational inefficiency. In this work, we propose a novel framework for efficient model aggregation called the early-stopped aggregation (ESA): instead of computing and aggregating estimators for all candidate models, we compute only a small number of simpler ones using an early-stopping criterion and aggregate only these for final inference. Our framework is versatile and applies to both Bayesian model selection, in particular, within the variational Bayes framework, and frequentist estimation, including a general penalized estimation setting.
  We investigate adaptive optimal property of the ESA approach across three learning paradigms. We first show that ESA achieves optimal adaptive contraction rates in the variational Bayes setting under mild conditions. We extend this result to variational empirical Bayes, where prior hyperparameters are chosen in a data-dependent manner. In addition, we apply the ESA approach to frequentist aggregation including both penalization-based and sample-splitting implementations, and establish corresponding theory. As we demonstrate, there is a clear unification between early-stopped Bayes and frequentist penalized aggregation, with a common "energy" functional comprising a data-fitting term and a complexity-control term that drives both procedures. We further present several applications and numerical studies that highlight the efficiency and strong performance of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [159] [Tweedie Calculus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14486)
*Santiago Torres*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个通用框架来研究加性噪声模型中的Tweedie表示，将寻找此类公式的问题转化为缓增分布微积分问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tweedie公式是测量误差分析和经验贝叶斯的基石，但除了少数经典例子外，缺乏系统方法来确定此类表示何时存在以及如何推导。需要建立通用框架来理解加性噪声模型中的后验泛函直接表达式

Method: 开发了加性噪声模型中Tweedie表示的通用框架，引入Tweedie泛函的线性映射概念，证明其存在性、唯一性和连续性。通过傅里叶变换将问题转化为缓增分布微积分，并扩展到异方差高斯序列模型

Result: 建立了Tweedie表示的存在性理论，恢复了经典高斯情况，扩展到广泛的噪声分布族，并在异方差高斯序列模型中通过变量变换获得了新的Tweedie表示

Conclusion: 该框架为加性噪声模型中的Tweedie型公式提供了系统理论，将问题重新表述为缓增分布微积分问题，扩展了经典结果并开辟了新的应用方向

Abstract: Tweedie's formula is a cornerstone of measurement-error analysis and empirical Bayes. In the Gaussian location model, it recovers posterior means directly from the observed marginal density, bypassing nonparametric deconvolution. Beyond a few classical examples, however, there is no systematic method for determining when such representations exist or how to derive them. This paper develops a general framework for such identities in additive-noise models. I study when posterior functionals admit direct expressions in terms of the observed density -- identities I call \emph{Tweedie representations} -- and show that they are characterized by a linear map, the \emph{Tweedie functional}. Under general conditions, I establish its existence, uniqueness, and continuity. I further show that, in many applications, the Tweedie functional can be expressed as the inverse Fourier transform of an explicit tempered distribution, suitably extended when necessary. This reframes the search for Tweedie-type formulas as a problem in the calculus of tempered distributions. The framework recovers the classical Gaussian case and extends to a broad family of noise distributions for which such representations were previously unavailable. It also goes beyond the standard additive model: in the heteroskedastic Gaussian sequence model, a change of variables restores the required structure conditionally and yields new Tweedie representations.

</details>


### [160] [On general weighted cumulative residual (past) extropy of extreme order statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15061)
*Santosh Kumar Chaudhary,Sarikul Islam,Nitin Gupta*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出加权累积残差外熵(GWCREx)和加权累积过去外熵(GWCPEx)作为极端顺序统计量的信息度量，建立了分布唯一性表征，并针对广义帕累托分布和幂分布给出了具体表征结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加权外熵作为量化不确定性的灵活信息度量在顺序统计量中具有重要应用，需要将其扩展到累积框架以分析极端顺序统计量，为可靠性工程和生存分析提供统一的信息论工具。

Method: 定义最小顺序统计量的广义加权累积残差外熵(GWCREx)和最大顺序统计量的广义加权累积过去外熵(GWCPEx)，包括它们的动态版本，证明这些加权度量及其动态对应物能唯一表征基础分布。

Result: 建立了加权累积外熵度量与分布之间的唯一性关系，针对广义帕累托分布和幂分布这两个广泛使用的可靠性模型给出了新的表征结果，为极端寿命分析提供了信息论框架。

Conclusion: 提出的加权累积外熵框架为分析可靠性工程和生存分析中的极端寿命提供了统一的信息论工具，扩展了加权外熵在顺序统计量中的应用范围，具有理论和应用价值。

Abstract: Weighted extropy has recently emerged as a flexible information measure for quantifying uncertainty, with particular relevance to order statistics. In this paper, we introduce and study a weighted cumulative analogue of extropy, extending the framework of weighted cumulative residual and cumulative past entropies to extreme order statistics. Specifically, we define the general weighted cumulative residual extropy (GWCREx) for the smallest order statistic and the general weighted cumulative past extropy (GWCPEx) for the largest order statistic, along with their dynamic versions. We show that these weighted measures and their dynamic counterparts uniquely characterize the underlying distribution. Moreover, we establish new characterization results for two widely used reliability models: the generalized Pareto distribution and the power distribution. The proposed framework provides a unified information-theoretic tool for analysing extreme lifetimes in reliability engineering and survival analysis.

</details>


### [161] [On a Probability Inequality for Order Statistics with Applications to Bootstrap, Conformal Prediction, and more](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15229)
*Manit Paul,Arun Kumar Kuchibhotla*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 论文探讨了概率不等式在近似条件下的扩展，特别是当随机变量不独立或非同分布时，P(X ≤ X') ≈ 1/2的近似不等式，并展示了其在重抽样推断、保形预测、置换检验和秩检验中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Kolmogorov"每个极限定理背后都有一个不等式"的启发，研究当独立同分布假设被违反时，概率不等式P(X ≤ X') ≥ 1/2如何推广到近似条件。探索当随机变量不独立或非同分布时，该概率是否仍近似为1/2，为统计推断提供理论基础。

Method: 扩展经典概率不等式P(X ≤ X') ≥ 1/2（对独立同分布随机变量成立）到近似条件：1）当X和X'独立但非同分布时，若分布近似相等，则概率近似1/2；2）当X和X'不独立时，若近似独立，则概率也近似1/2。进一步将不等式扩展到顺序统计量，并建立违反独立性和同分布假设下的近似不等式理论。

Result: 证明了近似不等式在多种统计方法中的有效性：1）为自助法/子抽样的渐近有效性提供理论基础；2）证明保形预测的有限样本有效性；3）验证置换检验的有效性；4）证明无群不变性条件下秩检验的渐近有效性。在重抽样推断中，这些结果可视为Peter Hall某些结果的有限样本实现，并提供了一种适用于高维数据的"廉价自助法"。

Conclusion: 每个不等式在近似正则条件下都有对应的近似不等式。论文建立的近似概率不等式框架为统计推断提供了强大的理论基础，特别是在假设条件被违反时仍能保持近似有效性。这一理论统一了多种统计方法，包括自助法、保形预测、置换检验和秩检验，并为高维数据分析提供了实用的"廉价自助法"工具。

Abstract: ``Behind every limit theorem, there is an inequality'' said Kolmogorov. We say ``for every inequality, there is an approximate inequality under approximate regularity conditions.'' Suppose $X, X'$ are independent and identically distributed random variables. Then $X \le X'$ with a probability of at least $1/2$, irrespective of the underlying (common) distribution. One can ask what happens to the probability if $X, X'$ are independent but not identically distributed. It should be approximately $1/2$ if the distributions are approximately equal. Similarly, what if the random variables are dependent? It should, again, be approximately $1/2$ if the random variables are approximately independent. We explore an extension of this probability inequality involving order statistics and develop approximate versions of such an inequality under violations of independence and identical distribution assumptions. We further show that this inequality can be used as a basis to prove asymptotic validity of bootstrap/subsampling, finite-sample validity of conformal prediction, permutation tests, and asymptotic validity of rank tests without group invariance. Specifically, in the context of resampling inference, our results can be seen as a finite-sample instantiation of some results by Peter Hall and yield an alternative ``cheap bootstrap'' that applies to high-dimensional data.

</details>


### [162] [Generalization of Pearl's Front-Door Criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15288)
*Carol Wu,Elina Robeva*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文提出了新的、弱化的图基条件，扩展了前门准则在存在潜在混杂情况下估计总因果效应的适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pearl的前门准则为在存在潜在混杂的情况下从观测数据估计总因果效应提供了一组充分条件。一个开放问题是这些条件是否可以推广为既必要又充分，类似于Shpitser对后门调整准则的推广。本文旨在探索前门准则的推广可能性。

Method: 提出了一组新的、弱化的图基条件，这些条件对于前门公式估计总因果效应是充分的。这些条件扩展了原始前门准则的适用范围，使更多问题能够通过前门识别方法解决。

Result: 成功推导出比原始前门准则更弱的充分条件，扩大了前门公式适用的因果图范围，使更多存在潜在混杂的因果效应估计问题能够通过前门方法解决。

Conclusion: 本文扩展了前门准则的理论基础，提供了更弱的充分条件，增加了前门识别方法的适用性，为在更广泛的实际问题中应用前门准则进行因果效应估计奠定了基础。

Abstract: Pearl's front-door criterion provides a set of sufficient conditions for estimating the total causal effect from observational data in the presence of latent confounding, using the functional P(y | do(x := x*)) = \sum_z P(z | x*) \sum_x P(y | x, z) P(x). An open question is whether these conditions can be generalized to be both necessary and sufficient for the validity of this functional, similar to the generalization achieved for the back-door adjustment criterion by Shpitser. In this paper, we present a new, weakened set of graph-based conditions sufficient for the front-door formula to estimate the total causal effect, expanding the scope of problems amenable to front-door identification.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [163] [The Devil Is in Gradient Entanglement: Energy-Aware Gradient Coordinator for Robust Generalized Category Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14176)
*Haiyang Zheng,Nan Pu,Yaqi Cai,Teng Long,Wenjing Li,Nicu Sebe,Zhun Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EAGC（能量感知梯度协调器）解决GCD中梯度纠缠问题，通过锚定梯度对齐和能量感知弹性投影来优化已知类与未知类的表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义类别发现（GCD）利用标记数据对来自已知或未知类别的未标记样本进行分类。现有方法通常联合优化监督和无监督目标，但存在优化干扰问题。通过定量分析发现梯度纠缠问题：1）扭曲监督梯度，削弱已知类间的区分性；2）导致已知类与未知类表示子空间重叠，降低未知类别的可分离性。

Method: 提出能量感知梯度协调器（EAGC），包含两个组件：1）基于锚点的梯度对齐（AGA）：引入参考模型锚定标记样本的梯度方向，保护已知类的判别结构免受未标记梯度干扰；2）能量感知弹性投影（EEP）：将未标记梯度软投影到已知类子空间的补空间，并基于能量系数自适应缩放投影，根据每个未标记样本与已知子空间的对齐程度调整，减少子空间重叠同时不过度抑制可能属于已知类的未标记样本。

Result: 实验表明EAGC能持续提升现有方法性能，并建立新的最先进结果。代码已开源。

Conclusion: EAGC通过梯度层面的显式优化调节，有效解决了GCD中的梯度纠缠问题，提高了已知类和未知类的表示学习质量，为广义类别发现提供了有效的优化框架。

Abstract: Generalized Category Discovery (GCD) leverages labeled data to categorize unlabeled samples from known or unknown classes. Most previous methods jointly optimize supervised and unsupervised objectives and achieve promising results. However, inherent optimization interference still limits their ability to improve further. Through quantitative analysis, we identify a key issue, i.e., gradient entanglement, which 1) distorts supervised gradients and weakens discrimination among known classes, and 2) induces representation-subspace overlap between known and novel classes, reducing the separability of novel categories. To address this issue, we propose the Energy-Aware Gradient Coordinator (EAGC), a plug-and-play gradient-level module that explicitly regulates the optimization process. EAGC comprises two components: Anchor-based Gradient Alignment (AGA) and Energy-aware Elastic Projection (EEP). AGA introduces a reference model to anchor the gradient directions of labeled samples, preserving the discriminative structure of known classes against the interference of unlabeled gradients. EEP softly projects unlabeled gradients onto the complement of the known-class subspace and derives an energy-based coefficient to adaptively scale the projection for each unlabeled sample according to its degree of alignment with the known subspace, thereby reducing subspace overlap without suppressing unlabeled samples that likely belong to known classes. Experiments show that EAGC consistently boosts existing methods and establishes new state-of-the-art results. Code is available at https://haiyangzheng.github.io/EAGC.

</details>


### [164] [Towards Verified and Targeted Explanations through Formal Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14209)
*Hanchen David Wang,Diego Manzanas Lopez,Preston K. Robinette,Ipek Oguz,Taylor T. Johnson,Meiyi Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ViTaX：首个提供形式化保证的针对性解释框架，通过目标ε鲁棒性认证模型对特定关键替代类别的抵抗能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法存在缺陷：启发式归因技术（如LIME、积分梯度）缺乏数学保证，而形式化方法虽然验证鲁棒性但非针对性分析。在安全关键系统中，不同误分类的后果差异巨大，需要针对特定关键替代类别提供形式化保证的解释。

Method: ViTaX框架包含两个核心步骤：(1) 识别对y→t转换最敏感的最小特征子集；(2) 应用形式化可达性分析，保证在ε扰动下这些特征不会导致分类翻转到目标类别t。通过目标ε鲁棒性形式化认证特征子集在朝向特定目标类别扰动下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在MNIST、GTSRB、EMNIST和TaxiNet数据集上的评估显示，ViTaX在最小解释基数下实现了超过30%的保真度提升，是首个提供模型对用户指定替代类别抵抗能力形式化保证的解释方法。

Conclusion: ViTaX填补了现有XAI方法的空白，为安全关键应用提供了具有数学保证的针对性半事实解释，能够识别模型对特定高风险误分类的抵抗能力，为可信AI部署提供了重要工具。

Abstract: As deep neural networks are deployed in safety-critical domains such as autonomous driving and medical diagnosis, stakeholders need explanations that are interpretable but also trustworthy with formal guarantees. Existing XAI methods fall short: heuristic attribution techniques (e.g., LIME, Integrated Gradients) highlight influential features but offer no mathematical guarantees about decision boundaries, while formal methods verify robustness yet remain untargeted, analyzing the nearest boundary regardless of whether it represents a critical risk. In safety-critical systems, not all misclassifications carry equal consequences; confusing a "Stop" sign for a "60 kph" sign is far more dangerous than confusing it with a "No Passing" sign. We introduce ViTaX (Verified and Targeted Explanations), a formal XAI framework that generates targeted semifactual explanations with mathematical guarantees. For a given input (class y) and a user-specified critical alternative (class t), ViTaX: (1) identifies the minimal feature subset most sensitive to the y->t transition, and (2) applies formal reachability analysis to guarantee that perturbing these features by epsilon cannot flip the classification to t. We formalize this through Targeted epsilon-Robustness, certifying whether a feature subset remains robust under perturbation toward a specific target class. ViTaX is the first method to provide formally guaranteed explanations of a model's resilience against user-identified alternatives. Evaluations on MNIST, GTSRB, EMNIST, and TaxiNet demonstrate over 30% fidelity improvement with minimal explanation cardinality.

</details>


### [165] [Metric-Aware Principal Component Analysis (MAPCA):A Unified Framework for Scale-Invariant Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14249)
*Michael Leznik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MAPCA是一个基于广义特征问题的尺度不变表示学习统一框架，通过度量矩阵M控制表示几何，将PCA、IPCA、Barlow Twins、VICReg、W-MSE等方法统一到同一理论框架中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示学习方法如PCA、IPCA、Barlow Twins、VICReg、W-MSE等看似不同，但缺乏统一的几何理论框架来解释它们之间的关系和本质区别。作者希望建立一个统一的数学框架，将这些方法系统化地联系起来，并揭示它们之间的几何联系和尺度不变性条件。

Method: 提出Metric-Aware PCA (MAPCA)框架，基于广义特征问题：最大化Tr(W^T ΣW)，约束条件为W^T M W = I，其中M是对称正定度量矩阵。通过选择不同的M实现不同的表示几何：1) β族度量M(β)=Σ^β，β∈[0,1]，提供从标准PCA(β=0)到输出白化(β=1)的连续谱偏置控制；2) 对角度量M=D=diag(Σ)恢复IPCA；3) 分析不同自监督学习目标在MAPCA框架中的对应关系。

Result: 1) 建立了统一的表示学习框架，将多种方法系统化联系；2) 证明了尺度不变性当且仅当度量变换为M_tilde=CMC，IPCA精确满足此条件而β族在中间值不满足；3) 发现Barlow Twins和ZCA白化对应β=1(输出白化)，VICReg方差项对应对角度量，W-MSE对应M=Σ^{-1}(β=-1)，处于谱压缩范围之外且与Barlow Twins方向相反；4) 揭示了输入白化和输出白化的区别仅在MAPCA框架下才变得精确。

Conclusion: MAPCA提供了一个统一的几何语言，将看似不同的表示学习方法系统化地联系起来，揭示了它们之间的本质区别和联系。该框架不仅统一了经典方法如PCA和IPCA，还统一了现代自监督学习目标，为理解表示学习中的几何结构提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We introduce Metric-Aware Principal Component Analysis (MAPCA), a unified framework for scale-invariant representation learning based on the generalised eigenproblem max Tr(W^T Sigma W) subject to W^T M W = I, where M is a symmetric positive definite metric matrix. The choice of M determines the representation geometry. The canonical beta-family M(beta) = Sigma^beta, beta in [0,1], provides continuous spectral bias control between standard PCA (beta=0) and output whitening (beta=1), with condition number kappa(beta) = (lambda_1/lambda_p)^(1-beta) decreasing monotonically to isotropy. The diagonal metric M = D = diag(Sigma) recovers Invariant PCA (IPCA), a method rooted in Frisch (1928) diagonal regression, as a distinct member of the broader framework. We prove that scale invariance holds if and only if the metric transforms as M_tilde = CMC under rescaling C, a condition satisfied exactly by IPCA but not by the general beta-family at intermediate values.
  Beyond its classical interpretation, MAPCA provides a geometric language that unifies several self-supervised learning objectives. Barlow Twins and ZCA whitening correspond to beta=1 (output whitening); VICReg's variance term corresponds to the diagonal metric. A key finding is that W-MSE, despite being described as a whitening-based method, corresponds to M = Sigma^{-1} (beta = -1), outside the spectral compression range entirely and in the opposite spectral direction to Barlow Twins. This distinction between input and output whitening is invisible at the level of loss functions and becomes precise only within the MAPCA framework.

</details>


### [166] [Heat and Matérn Kernels on Matchings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14331)
*Dmitry Eremeev,Salem Said,Viacheslav Borovitskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出匹配空间的几何核框架，通过平稳核、热核和Matérn核扩展欧几里得核到匹配空间，开发子指数算法进行高效计算，并探索向树空间的迁移


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于匹配具有离散、非欧几里得的特性，将核方法应用于匹配具有挑战性。需要构建尊重匹配空间自然几何结构的核方法框架

Method: 1) 完全刻画平稳核（尊重空间对称性）；2) 聚焦热核和Matérn核族，在平稳性基础上加入平滑性先验；3) 开发基于zonal多项式的子指数算法进行高效核评估；4) 探索框架向树空间的迁移

Result: 成功将广泛使用的欧几里得核扩展到匹配空间，但朴素评估具有超指数计算成本。提出的zonal多项式算法实现了子指数计算复杂度。在向树空间迁移方面建立了新的负面结果

Conclusion: 构建了匹配空间的几何核框架，通过平稳性和平滑性先验扩展了热核和Matérn核，开发了高效计算算法。向树空间的迁移存在挑战，识别了重要的开放性问题

Abstract: Applying kernel methods to matchings is challenging due to their discrete, non-Euclidean nature. In this paper, we develop a principled framework for constructing geometric kernels that respect the natural geometry of the space of matchings. To this end, we first provide a complete characterization of stationary kernels, i.e. kernels that respect the inherent symmetries of this space. Because the class of stationary kernels is too broad, we specifically focus on the heat and Matérn kernel families, adding an appropriate inductive bias of smoothness to stationarity. While these families successfully extend widely popular Euclidean kernels to matchings, evaluating them naively incurs a prohibitive super-exponential computational cost. To overcome this difficulty, we introduce and analyze a novel, sub-exponential algorithm leveraging zonal polynomials for efficient kernel evaluation. Finally, motivated by the known bijective correspondence between matchings and phylogenetic trees-a crucial data modality in biology-we explore whether our framework can be seamlessly transferred to the space of trees, establishing novel negative results and identifying a significant open problem.

</details>


### [167] [Path-Sampled Integrated Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14338)
*Firuz Kamalov,Fadi Thabtah,R. Sivaraj,Neda Abdelhamid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出路径采样积分梯度（PS-IG）框架，通过沿线性插值路径采样基线计算期望值来泛化特征归因，证明其与路径加权积分梯度数学等价，显著提高误差收敛速度并降低方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征归因方法存在误差收敛速度慢（O(m^{-1/2})）和梯度噪声导致的方差问题，需要一种既能保持公理性质又能提高计算效率和稳定性的归因框架。

Method: 提出路径采样积分梯度（PS-IG）框架，通过沿线性插值路径均匀采样基线计算期望值；证明当加权函数匹配采样密度的累积分布函数时，PS-IG与路径加权积分梯度数学等价，从而可将随机期望转化为确定性黎曼和计算。

Result: PS-IG将误差收敛速度从O(m^{-1/2})提升到O(m^{-1})；在均匀采样下严格降低归因方差1/3；同时保持线性、实现不变性等关键公理性质；作为梯度噪声的方差降低滤波器。

Conclusion: PS-IG提供了一种理论严谨的特征归因框架，通过路径采样将随机期望转化为确定性计算，显著提高了收敛速度和稳定性，同时保持了重要的公理性质，为可解释AI提供了更可靠的归因方法。

Abstract: We introduce path-sampled integrated gradients (PS-IG), a framework that generalizes feature attribution by computing the expected value over baselines sampled along the linear interpolation path. We prove that PS-IG is mathematically equivalent to path-weighted integrated gradients, provided the weighting function matches the cumulative distribution function of the sampling density. This equivalence allows the stochastic expectation to be evaluated via a deterministic Riemann sum, improving the error convergence rate from $O(m^{-1/2})$ to $O(m^{-1})$ for smooth models. Furthermore, we demonstrate analytically that PS-IG functions as a variance-reducing filter against gradient noise - strictly lowering attribution variance by a factor of 1/3 under uniform sampling - while preserving key axiomatic properties such as linearity and implementation invariance.

</details>


### [168] [CLion: Efficient Cautious Lion Optimizer with Enhanced Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14587)
*Feihu Huang,Guanyi Zhang,Songcan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Lion优化器的泛化性能，通过数学归纳法证明其泛化误差为O(1/(Nτ^T))，并提出新的CLion优化器，具有更低的泛化误差O(1/N)和O(√d/T^{1/4})的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lion优化器在深度学习训练中表现出色，但其收敛性已有研究而泛化分析仍缺失。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究Lion的泛化特性，并设计具有更好泛化性能的改进优化器。

Method: 采用基于数学归纳法的算法稳定性分析来研究Lion的泛化性能。设计新的CLion优化器，通过谨慎使用符号函数来增强泛化能力。同时分析CLion的收敛性质，证明其在非凸随机优化下的收敛速率。

Result: 证明Lion优化器的泛化误差为O(1/(Nτ^T))，其中τ为梯度估计器中非零元素的最小绝对值。发现SignSGD具有与Lion相同的泛化误差。提出的CLion优化器具有更低的泛化误差O(1/N)，并在非凸随机优化下达到O(√d/T^{1/4})的收敛速率。

Conclusion: 本文首次对Lion优化器进行泛化分析，填补了理论空白。提出的CLion优化器在保持高效性的同时，显著改善了泛化性能，并通过实验验证了其有效性。这项工作为理解基于符号的优化算法的泛化特性提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Lion optimizer is a popular learning-based optimization algorithm in machine learning, which shows impressive performance in training many deep learning models. Although convergence property of the Lion optimizer has been studied, its generalization analysis is still missing. To fill this gap, we study generalization property of the Lion via algorithmic stability based on the mathematical induction. Specifically, we prove that the Lion has a generalization error of $O(\frac{1}{Nτ^T})$, where $N$ is training sample size, and $τ>0$ denotes the smallest absolute value of non-zero element in gradient estimator, and $T$ is the total iteration number. In addition, we obtain an interesting byproduct that the SignSGD algorithm has the same generalization error as the Lion. To enhance generalization of the Lion, we design a novel efficient Cautious Lion (i.e., CLion) optimizer by cautiously using sign function. Moreover, we prove that our CLion has a lower generalization error of $O(\frac{1}{N})$ than $O(\frac{1}{Nτ^T})$ of the Lion, since the parameter $τ$ generally is very small. Meanwhile, we study convergence property of our CLion optimizer, and prove that our CLion has a fast convergence rate of $O(\frac{\sqrt{d}}{T^{1/4}})$ under $\ell_1$-norm of gradient for nonconvex stochastic optimization, where $d$ denotes the model dimension.
  Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate effectiveness of our CLion optimizer.

</details>


### [169] [Gating Enables Curvature: A Geometric Expressivity Gap in Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14702)
*Satwik Bathula,Anand A. Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过统计几何分析发现，带门控的注意力机制能产生非平坦几何结构（包括正曲率流形），而无门控注意力仅限于平坦流形，这解释了门控注意力在需要非线性决策边界任务上的性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管门控注意力在大型语言模型中已成功应用并改善了性能和训练稳定性，但其数学机制仍缺乏深入理解。研究者旨在通过统计几何方法揭示门控与非门控注意力在表示能力上的根本差异。

Method: 将注意力输出建模为高斯分布的均值参数，分析其诱导的Fisher-Rao几何。通过统计流形几何理论，比较无门控（仿射结构）与带乘法门控的注意力机制在几何表达能力上的差异。

Result: 无门控注意力限于内在平坦的统计流形，而乘法门控能产生非平坦几何（包括正曲率流形）。实验表明，门控模型具有更高的表示曲率，在需要非线性决策边界的任务上表现更好，但在线性任务上无一致优势。还发现了曲率在组合下积累的结构化机制，产生系统性的深度放大效应。

Conclusion: 门控注意力在几何表达能力上显著优于无门控注意力，能够产生更丰富的统计流形结构，这解释了其在复杂任务上的性能优势。几何分析为理解注意力机制的表达能力提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Multiplicative gating is widely used in neural architectures and has recently been applied to attention layers to improve performance and training stability in large language models. Despite the success of gated attention, the mathematical implications of gated attention mechanisms remain poorly understood. We study attention through the geometry of its representations by modeling outputs as mean parameters of Gaussian distributions and analyzing the induced Fisher--Rao geometry. We show that ungated attention operator is restricted to intrinsically flat statistical manifolds due to its affine structure, while multiplicative gating enables non-flat geometries, including positively curved manifolds that are unattainable in the ungated setting. These results establish a geometric expressivity gap between ungated and gated attention. Empirically, we show that gated models exhibit higher representation curvature and improved performance on tasks requiring nonlinear decision boundaries whereas they provide no consistent advantage on tasks with linear decision boundaries. Furthermore, we identify a structured regime in which curvature accumulates under composition, yielding a systematic depth amplification effect.

</details>


### [170] [Multi-User mmWave Beam and Rate Adaptation via Combinatorial Satisficing Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14908)
*Emre Özyıldırım,Barış Yaycı,Umut Eren Akturk,Cem Tekin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出SAT-CTS策略，用于毫米波MISO系统中的波束和速率自适应，通过满足性目标而非最大化目标来优化多用户服务质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多用户毫米波MISO系统中，基站使用有限码本的模拟波束成形为多个单天线用户设备服务，每个用户有独特的波束和离散数据传输速率。基站通过ACK/NACK反馈了解传输成功情况。需要设计一种轻量级策略，能够在不了解信道状态信息的情况下，通过学习波束-速率组合来满足服务质量目标，而非仅仅最大化吞吐量。

Method: 引入满足性吞吐量阈值τ_r，将联合波束和速率自适应建模为波束-速率元组的组合半赌博问题。提出SAT-CTS策略，该策略结合保守置信度估计和后验采样，引导学习过程满足τ_r而非最大化目标。策略包含两种模式：当τ_r可实现时，策略收敛到满足阈值；当τ_r不可实现时，策略在有限期望瞬态后重启CTS轮次。

Result: 理论贡献：首次为具有满足性目标的组合半赌博问题提供有限时间遗憾界。当τ_r可实现时，对目标的累积满足性遗憾上界为时间无关常数；当τ_r不可实现时，SAT-CTS仅产生有限期望瞬态，之后其遗憾由重启CTS轮次的遗憾贡献之和决定，获得O((log T)^2)的标准遗憾界。实验结果表明，SAT-CTS在时变稀疏多径信道中持续减少满足性遗憾，保持竞争力的标准遗憾，同时实现有利的平均吞吐量和用户间公平性。

Conclusion: SAT-CTS策略能够在不了解信道状态信息的情况下，通过反馈高效学习公平分配波束和速率以满足服务质量目标。该策略在满足性目标下表现出色，同时保持标准遗憾性能，为毫米波系统中的自适应资源分配提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: We study downlink beam and rate adaptation in a multi-user mmWave MISO system where multiple base stations (BSs), each using analog beamforming from finite codebooks, serve multiple single-antenna user equipments (UEs) with a unique beam per UE and discrete data transmission rates. BSs learn about transmission success based on ACK/NACK feedback. To encode service goals, we introduce a satisficing throughput threshold $τ_r$ and cast joint beam and rate adaptation as a combinatorial semi-bandit over beam-rate tuples. Within this framework, we propose SAT-CTS, a lightweight, threshold-aware policy that blends conservative confidence estimates with posterior sampling, steering learning toward meeting $τ_r$ rather than merely maximizing. Our main theoretical contribution provides the first finite-time regret bounds for combinatorial semi-bandits with satisficing objective: when $τ_r$ is realizable, we upper bound the cumulative satisficing regret to the target with a time-independent constant, and when $τ_r$ is non-realizable, we show that SAT-CTS incurs only a finite expected transient outside committed CTS rounds, after which its regret is governed by the sum of the regret contributions of restarted CTS rounds, yielding an $O((\log T)^2)$ standard regret bound. On the practical side, we evaluate the performance via cumulative satisficing regret to $τ_r$ alongside standard regret and fairness. Experiments with time-varying sparse multipath channels show that SAT-CTS consistently reduces satisficing regret and maintains competitive standard regret, while achieving favorable average throughput and fairness across users, indicating that feedback-efficient learning can equitably allocate beams and rates to meet QoS targets without channel state knowledge.

</details>


### [171] [No More Guessing: a Verifiable Gradient Inversion Attack in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15063)
*Francesco Diana,Chuan Xu,André Nusser,Giovanni Neglia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可验证梯度反演攻击(VGIA)，通过代数验证测试确保重构样本的正确性，挑战表格数据不易受攻击的认知


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有梯度反演攻击在重构多记录聚合梯度时可能失败且无法验证正确性，表格数据被认为较不易受攻击，但缺乏验证机制

Method: 采用ReLU泄漏的几何视角，利用全连接层激活边界定义输入空间超平面，引入基于子空间的代数验证测试检测单记录隔离区域，通过解析恢复特征向量和轻量优化重构目标

Result: 在大型批处理量的表格基准测试中实现精确记录和目标恢复，在现有最先进攻击失败或无法评估重构保真度的场景中表现优异，比先前几何方法更有效地分配超平面查询，实现更快重构和更少攻击轮次

Conclusion: 表格数据同样易受梯度反演攻击，VGIA提供可验证的正确性证书，挑战了表格数据隐私性更强的认知，为联邦学习隐私保护提出新挑战

Abstract: Gradient inversion attacks threaten client privacy in federated learning by reconstructing training samples from clients' shared gradients. Gradients aggregate contributions from multiple records and existing attacks may fail to disentangle them, yielding incorrect reconstructions with no intrinsic way to certify success. In vision and language, attackers may fall back on human inspection to judge reconstruction plausibility, but this is far less feasible for numerical tabular records, fueling the impression that tabular data is less vulnerable.
  We challenge this perception by proposing a verifiable gradient inversion attack (VGIA) that provides an explicit certificate of correctness for reconstructed samples. Our method adopts a geometric view of ReLU leakage: the activation boundary of a fully connected layer defines a hyperplane in input space. VGIA introduces an algebraic, subspace-based verification test that detects when a hyperplane-delimited region contains exactly one record. Once isolation is certified, VGIA recovers the corresponding feature vector analytically and reconstructs the target via a lightweight optimization step.
  Experiments on tabular benchmarks with large batch sizes demonstrate exact record and target recovery in regimes where existing state-of-the-art attacks either fail or cannot assess reconstruction fidelity. Compared to prior geometric approaches, VGIA allocates hyperplane queries more effectively, yielding faster reconstructions with fewer attack rounds.

</details>


### [172] [FedIDM: Achieving Fast and Stable Convergence in Byzantine Federated Learning through Iterative Distribution Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15115)
*He Yang,Dongyi Lv,Wei Xi,Song Ma,Hanlin Gu,Jizhong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedIDM：一种基于分布匹配的拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习方法，通过生成可信的压缩数据来识别和过滤异常客户端，实现快速稳定收敛


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习方法存在收敛慢、不稳定问题，且在处理大量恶意客户端时通常需要牺牲模型性能来保证鲁棒性

Method: FedIDM包含两个核心组件：1) 攻击容忍的压缩数据生成；2) 基于负贡献的鲁棒聚合与拒绝机制。通过分布匹配构建可信压缩数据，排除偏离压缩数据更新方向或导致压缩数据集显著损失的本地更新

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的综合评估表明，FedIDM在多种最先进的拜占庭攻击下，即使面对大量恶意客户端，也能实现快速稳定收敛并保持可接受的模型性能

Conclusion: FedIDM通过分布匹配和负贡献检测机制，有效解决了现有拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习方法收敛慢、不稳定以及在保证鲁棒性时牺牲模型性能的问题

Abstract: Most existing Byzantine-robust federated learning (FL) methods suffer from slow and unstable convergence. Moreover, when handling a substantial proportion of colluded malicious clients, achieving robustness typically entails compromising model utility. To address these issues, this work introduces FedIDM, which employs distribution matching to construct trustworthy condensed data for identifying and filtering abnormal clients. FedIDM consists of two main components: (1) attack-tolerant condensed data generation, and (2) robust aggregation with negative contribution-based rejection. These components exclude local updates that (1) deviate from the update direction derived from condensed data, or (2) cause a significant loss on the condensed dataset. Comprehensive evaluations on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that FedIDM achieves fast and stable convergence while maintaining acceptable model utility, under multiple state-of-the-art Byzantine attacks involving a large number of malicious clients.

</details>


### [173] [Shapley Value-Guided Adaptive Ensemble Learning for Explainable Financial Fraud Detection with U.S. Regulatory Compliance Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14231)
*Mohammad Nasir Uddin,Md Munna Aziz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究针对金融犯罪检测中AI模型缺乏可解释性的问题，提出SHAP引导自适应集成方法(SGAE)，在IEEE-CIS数据集上评估多种模型，将结果映射到监管合规要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融犯罪每年给美国机构造成超过320亿美元损失。虽然AI欺诈检测工具日益先进，但在实际应用中面临主要障碍：许多模型作为黑箱运行，无法提供OCC 2011-12公告和美联储SR 11-7等法规要求的透明、可审计的解释。

Method: 1. 全面评估解释质量，包括忠实性（k=5,10,15时的充分性和全面性）和稳定性（30个bootstrap样本的Kendall's W）；2. 提出SHAP引导自适应集成(SGAE)，基于SHAP归因一致性动态调整每笔交易的集成权重；3. 在完整的590,540笔交易的IEEE-CIS数据集上对LSTM、Transformer和GNN-GraphSAGE三种架构进行完整评估。

Result: 1. XGBoost与TreeExplainer组合达到近乎完美的稳定性(W=0.9912)，而LSTM与DeepExplainer组合表现较弱(W=0.4962)；2. SGAE在所有测试模型中达到最高的AUC-ROC（保留集0.8837；交叉验证0.9245）；3. GNN-GraphSAGE在完整数据集上表现最佳，AUC-ROC为0.9248，F1分数为0.6013。所有结果直接映射到OCC、SR 11-7和BSA-AML监管合规要求。

Conclusion: 该研究通过系统评估解释质量、提出自适应集成方法以及全面比较三种深度学习架构，为金融犯罪检测提供了既高性能又符合监管要求的可解释AI解决方案，特别是GNN-GraphSAGE和SGAE方法在准确性和可解释性方面表现出色。

Abstract: Financial crime costs U.S. institutions over $32 billion each year. Although AI tools for fraud detection have become more advanced, their use in real-world systems still faces a major obstacle: many of these models operate as black boxes that cannot provide the transparent, auditable explanations required by regulations such as OCC Bulletin 2011-12 and Federal Reserve SR 11-7. This study makes three main contributions. First, it offers a thorough evaluation of explanation quality across faithfulness (sufficiency and comprehensiveness at k=5, 10, and 15) and stability (Kendall's W across 30 bootstrap samples). XGBoost paired with TreeExplainer achieves near-perfect stability (W=0.9912), while LSTM with DeepExplainer shows weak results (W=0.4962). Second, the paper introduces the SHAP-Guided Adaptive Ensemble (SGAE), which dynamically adjusts per-transaction ensemble weights based on SHAP attribution agreement, achieving the highest AUC-ROC among all tested models (0.8837 held-out; 0.9245 cross-validation). Third, a complete three-architecture evaluation of LSTM, Transformer, and GNN-GraphSAGE on the full 590,540-transaction IEEE-CIS dataset is provided, with GNN-GraphSAGE achieving AUC-ROC 0.9248 and F1=0.6013. All results are mapped directly to OCC, SR 11-7, and BSA-AML regulatory compliance requirements.

</details>


### [174] [TOPCELL: Topology Optimization of Standard Cell via LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14237)
*Zhan Song,Yu-Tung Liu,Chen Chen,Guoheng Sun,Jiaqi Yin,Chia-tung Ho,Ang Li,Haoxing Ren,Cunxi Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TOPCELL：基于大语言模型的晶体管拓扑优化框架，通过生成式方法解决标准单元设计中高维拓扑搜索难题，在2nm工艺节点上显著优于基础模型，在7nm库生成任务中实现85.91倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体管拓扑优化是标准单元设计的关键步骤，直接影响扩散共享效率和下游布线能力。随着先进工艺节点电路复杂度增加，传统穷举搜索方法计算不可行，需要新的可扩展解决方案。

Method: 提出TOPCELL框架，将高维拓扑探索重新表述为生成任务，使用大语言模型进行拓扑优化。采用组相对策略优化（GRPO）微调模型，使其拓扑优化策略与逻辑（电路）和空间（布局）约束对齐。

Result: 在面向2nm先进工艺节点的工业流程中，TOPCELL在发现可布线、物理感知拓扑方面显著优于基础模型。在7nm库生成任务中，TOPCELL展示了强大的零样本泛化能力，匹配穷举求解器的布局质量，同时实现85.91倍加速。

Conclusion: TOPCELL提供了一个新颖且可扩展的框架，通过大语言模型和GRPO微调，有效解决了晶体管拓扑优化的计算瓶颈问题，在先进工艺节点标准单元设计中展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Transistor topology optimization is a critical step in standard cell design, directly dictating diffusion sharing efficiency and downstream routability. However, identifying optimal topologies remains a persistent bottleneck, as conventional exhaustive search methods become computationally intractable with increasing circuit complexity in advanced nodes. This paper introduces TOPCELL, a novel and scalable framework that reformulates high-dimensional topology exploration as a generative task using Large Language Models (LLMs). We employ Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to fine-tune the model, aligning its topology optimization strategy with logical (circuit) and spatial (layout) constraints. Experimental results within an industrial flow targeting an advanced 2nm technology node demonstrate that TOPCELL significantly outperforms foundation models in discovering routable, physically-aware topologies. When integrated into a state-of-the-art (SOTA) automation flow for a 7nm library generation task, TOPCELL exhibits robust zero-shot generalization and matches the layout quality of exhaustive solvers while achieving an 85.91x speedup.

</details>


### [175] [Optimistic Policy Learning under Pessimistic Adversaries with Regret and Violation Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14243)
*Sourav Ganguly,Kartik Pandit,Arnob Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究在显式对抗动态下的安全约束强化学习，提出RHC-UCRL算法处理外部因素对状态转移的影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实决策系统中，状态转移不仅取决于智能体动作，还受外部因素（竞争对手、环境扰动、战略对手）影响。忽略这些因素可能导致策略在孤立环境中最优但在部署时灾难性失败，特别是在需要满足安全约束的场景中。

Method: 将外部因素建模为对抗策略π̄，共同决定状态转移。提出Robust Hallucinated Constrained Upper-Confidence RL (RHC-UCRL)算法，这是一种基于模型的方法，同时对智能体和对抗策略保持乐观，明确区分认知不确定性和偶然不确定性。

Result: RHC-UCRL算法实现了次线性后悔和约束违反保证，为在显式对抗动态下的安全约束强化学习提供了理论保证。

Conclusion: 这是首个研究显式对抗动态下安全约束强化学习的工作，提出的RHC-UCRL算法能够处理外部因素对状态转移的影响，确保智能体在面对对抗时既保持最优性又满足安全约束。

Abstract: Real-world decision-making systems operate in environments where state transitions depend not only on the agent's actions, but also on \textbf{exogenous factors outside its control}--competing agents, environmental disturbances, or strategic adversaries--formally, $s_{h+1} = f(s_h, a_h, \bar{a}_h)+ω_h$ where $\bar{a}_h$ is the adversary/external action, $a_h$ is the agent's action, and $ω_h$ is an additive noise. Ignoring such factors can yield policies that are optimal in isolation but \textbf{fail catastrophically in deployment}, particularly when safety constraints must be satisfied.
  Standard Constrained MDP formulations assume the agent is the sole driver of state evolution, an assumption that breaks down in safety-critical settings. Existing robust RL approaches address this via distributional robustness over transition kernels, but do not explicitly model the \textbf{strategic interaction} between agent and exogenous factor, and rely on strong assumptions about divergence from a known nominal model.
  We model the exogenous factor as an \textbf{adversarial policy} $\barπ$ that co-determines state transitions, and ask how an agent can remain both optimal and safe against such an adversary. \emph{To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to study safety-constrained RL under explicit adversarial dynamics}. We propose \textbf{Robust Hallucinated Constrained Upper-Confidence RL} (\texttt{RHC-UCRL}), a model-based algorithm that maintains optimism over both agent and adversary policies, explicitly separating epistemic from aleatoric uncertainty. \texttt{RHC-UCRL} achieves sub-linear regret and constraint violation guarantees.

</details>


### [176] [Awakening Dormant Experts:Counterfactual Routing to Mitigate MoE Hallucinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14246)
*Wentao Hu,Yanbo Zhai,Xiaohui Hu,Mingkuan Zhao,Shanhong yu,Xue Liu,Kaidong Yu,Shuangyong Song,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Counterfactual Routing (CoR)训练无关推理框架，通过反事实专家影响度量动态唤醒稀疏MoE模型中休眠的专家，提升长尾知识处理能力而不增加推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏混合专家模型在处理长尾知识时易产生幻觉，原因是静态Top-k路由倾向于高频模式而非罕见事实关联，导致拥有关键长尾知识的"专家专家"被分配低门控分数而处于"休眠"状态。

Method: 提出Counterfactual Routing (CoR)训练无关推理框架，结合层间扰动分析和反事实专家影响度量，在保持总激活数不变的情况下，动态将计算资源从语法主导层转移到知识密集层，通过虚拟消融检索因果决定性专家。

Result: 在TruthfulQA、FACTOR和TriviaQA上的广泛实验表明，CoR平均提升事实准确性3.1%，不增加推理预算，相比静态扩展策略建立了更优的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: CoR通过动态路由机制有效解决了稀疏MoE模型中的专家休眠问题，显著提升了长尾知识处理能力，为高效利用专家模型的计算资源提供了新范式。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have achieved remarkable scalability, yet they remain vulnerable to hallucinations, particularly when processing long-tail knowledge. We identify that this fragility stems from static Top-$k$ routing: routers tend to favor high-frequency patterns over rare factual associations. Consequently, ``specialist experts'' possessing critical long-tail knowledge are often assigned low gating scores and remain ``dormant'' -- under-prioritized for specific tokens despite their proven causal importance on other inputs. To address this, we propose Counterfactual Routing (CoR), a training-free inference framework designed to awaken these dormant experts. CoR integrates layer-wise perturbation analysis with the Counterfactual Expert Impact (CEI) metric to dynamically shift computational resources from syntax-dominant to knowledge-intensive layers while maintaining a constant total activation count, effectively retrieving causally decisive experts via virtual ablation. Extensive experiments on TruthfulQA, FACTOR, and TriviaQA demonstrate that CoR improves factual accuracy by 3.1\% on average without increasing the inference budget, establishing a superior Pareto frontier compared to static scaling strategies.

</details>


### [177] [Calibrate-Then-Delegate: Safety Monitoring with Risk and Budget Guarantees via Model Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14251)
*Edoardo Pona,Milad Kazemi,Mehran Hosseini,Yali Du,David Watson,Osvaldo Simeone,Nicola Paoletti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CTD是一种新型模型级联方法，通过预测委托价值而非不确定性来优化LLM安全监控的成本-准确性平衡，提供计算成本的概率保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于不确定性的委托方法存在缺陷：不确定性不能准确反映专家模型是否真正能纠正错误，导致委托效益不佳。需要一种能直接预测委托价值的方法来优化成本-准确性平衡。

Method: 提出Calibrate-Then-Delegate (CTD)方法：1) 设计委托价值(DV)探针，基于与安全探针相同的内部表示直接预测委托效益；2) 通过多重假设测试在校准数据上设置DV阈值，强制执行预算约束并提供有限样本保证；3) 支持实例级流式决策。

Result: 在四个安全数据集上的评估显示：CTD在所有预算水平上始终优于基于不确定性的委托方法，避免有害的过度委托，并能根据输入难度自适应分配预算，无需组标签。

Conclusion: CTD通过直接预测委托价值而非依赖不确定性，显著改进了LLM安全监控的级联方法，在保证计算成本概率约束的同时提高了委托效率。

Abstract: Monitoring LLM safety at scale requires balancing cost and accuracy: a cheap latent-space probe can screen every input, but hard cases should be escalated to a more expensive expert. Existing cascades delegate based on probe uncertainty, but uncertainty is a poor proxy for delegation benefit, as it ignores whether the expert would actually correct the error. To address this problem, we introduce Calibrate-Then-Delegate (CTD), a model-cascade approach that provides probabilistic guarantees on the computation cost while enabling instance-level (streaming) decisions. CTD builds on a novel delegation value (DV) probe, a lightweight model operating on the same internal representations as the safety probe that directly predicts the benefit of escalation. To enforce budget constraints, CTD calibrates a threshold on the DV signal using held-out data via multiple hypothesis testing, yielding finite-sample guarantees on the delegation rate. Evaluated on four safety datasets, CTD consistently outperforms uncertainty-based delegation at every budget level, avoids harmful over-delegation, and adapts budget allocation to input difficulty without requiring group labels.

</details>


### [178] [GUI-Perturbed: Domain Randomization Reveals Systematic Brittleness in GUI Grounding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14262)
*Yangyue Wang,Harshvardhan Sikka,Yash Mathur,Tony Zhou,Jinu Nyachhyon,Pranav Guruprasad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GUI-Perturbed框架通过独立扰动视觉场景和指令来评估GUI grounding模型的鲁棒性，发现现有模型在空间推理指令上表现显著下降，而当前基准测试因单次固定指令评估而未能检测此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前GUI grounding模型在标准基准测试中报告超过85%的准确率，但当指令需要空间推理而非直接元素命名时，性能下降27-56个百分点。现有基准测试因每个截图仅使用单一固定指令评估而未能发现这一问题。

Method: 引入GUI-Perturbed框架，通过独立变化视觉场景和指令来测量grounding鲁棒性。评估了来自同一架构谱系的三个7B模型，测试了关系指令、70%浏览器缩放和rank-8 LoRA微调等扰动因素。

Result: 关系指令导致所有模型系统性准确率崩溃；70%浏览器缩放产生统计显著性能下降；使用增强数据的rank-8 LoRA微调反而降低性能而非改善。通过独立轴扰动，GUI-Perturbed能够隔离空间推理、视觉鲁棒性和推理校准等特定能力轴的影响。

Conclusion: GUI-Perturbed提供了聚合基准测试无法提供的诊断信号，揭示了现有GUI grounding模型在空间推理和视觉鲁棒性方面的系统性弱点。作者发布了数据集、增强管道和微调模型。

Abstract: GUI grounding models report over 85% accuracy on standard benchmarks, yet drop 27-56 percentage points when instructions require spatial reasoning rather than direct element naming. Current benchmarks miss this because they evaluate each screenshot once with a single fixed instruction. We introduce GUI-Perturbed, a controlled perturbation framework that independently varies visual scenes and instructions to measure grounding robustness. Evaluating three 7B models from the same architecture lineage, we find that relational instructions cause systematic accuracy collapse across all models, a 70% browser zoom produces statistically significant degradation, and rank-8 LoRA fine-tuning with augmented data degrades performance rather than improving it. By perturbing along independent axes, GUI-Perturbed isolates which specific capability axes are affected-spatial reasoning, visual robustness, reasoning calibration-providing diagnostic signal that aggregate benchmarks cannot. We release the dataset, augmentation pipeline, and a fine-tuned model.

</details>


### [179] [Reinforcement Learning via Value Gradient Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14265)
*Haoran Xu,Kaiwen Hu,Somayeh Sojoudi,Amy Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VGF是一种用于行为正则化强化学习的新范式，将问题转化为最优传输问题，通过离散梯度流求解，无需显式策略参数化，在离线RL和LLM RL任务中取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行为正则化RL方法存在局限性：基于重参数化策略梯度的方法难以扩展到大型生成模型，而拒绝采样方法在试图超越行为支持时可能过于保守。需要一种可扩展且灵活的新方法来解决值函数过优化问题。

Method: VGF将行为正则化RL转化为最优传输问题，将参考分布映射到值函数诱导的最优策略分布。通过离散梯度流求解，其中值梯度引导从参考分布初始化的粒子。该方法通过控制传输预算隐式施加正则化，无需显式策略参数化。

Result: VGF在离线RL基准（D4RL、OGBench）和LLM RL任务上显著优于现有方法，达到最先进水平。该方法能够通过调整传输预算实现自适应测试时缩放。

Conclusion: VGF为行为正则化RL提供了一个可扩展、灵活且表达性强的新范式，通过最优传输和离散梯度流框架有效解决了现有方法的局限性，在多个领域展现出卓越性能。

Abstract: We study behavior-regularized reinforcement learning (RL), where regularization toward a reference distribution (the dataset in offline RL or the base model in LLM RL finetuning) is essential to prevent value over-optimization caused by erroneous out-of-distribution extrapolation. Existing methods either rely on reparameterized policy gradient, which are difficult to scale to large generative models, or on reject sampling, which can be overly conservative when attempting to move beyond the behavior support. In this paper, we propose Value Gradient Flow (VGF), a scalable new paradigm for behavior-regularized RL. VGF casts behavior-regularized RL as an optimal transport problem that maps the reference distribution to the value-induced optimal policy distribution. We solve this transport problem via discrete gradient flow, where value gradients guide particles initialized from the reference distribution. Our analysis shows that VGF imposes regularization implicitly by controlling the transport budget. VGF eliminates explicit policy parameterization while remaining expressive and flexible, this enables adaptive test-time scaling by adjusting the transport budget. Extensive experiments demonstrate that VGF significantly outperforms prior methods, achieving state-of-the-art results on offline RL benchmarks (D4RL, OGBench) and LLM RL tasks. Code and runs can be found at https://ryanxhr.github.io/vgf.

</details>


### [180] [When Missing Becomes Structure: Intent-Preserving Policy Completion from Financial KOL Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14333)
*Yuncong Liu,Yuan Wan,Zhou Jiang,Yao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KICL框架，使用离线强化学习将社交媒体KOL言论转化为可执行的交易策略，填补KOL表达的投资意图与具体执行决策之间的结构化空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上的关键意见领袖(KOL)言论常被用作投资指导，但将其转化为可执行的交易策略而不对未指定的执行决策注入假设仍是一个开放性问题。KOL言论存在结构化空白：KOL表达方向性意图（买/卖什么及原因），但系统性地未指定执行决策（何时、多少、多久）。

Method: 提出意图保持策略补全框架，将KOL言论视为部分交易策略，使用离线强化学习围绕KOL表达的意图补全缺失的执行决策。在YouTube和X平台的多模态KOL言论数据（2022-2025）上进行实验。

Result: KICL在两个平台上均实现最佳回报和夏普比率，同时保持零无支持入场和零方向反转。消融实验显示完整框架相比KOL对齐基线带来18.9%的回报提升。

Conclusion: KOL言论中的空白不是随机缺陷而是结构化分离，通过意图保持策略补全框架可以系统地将KOL投资指导转化为可执行的交易策略，实现显著的投资绩效提升。

Abstract: Key Opinion Leader (KOL) discourse on social media is widely consumed as investment guidance, yet turning it into executable trading strategies without injecting assumptions about unspecified execution decisions remains an open problem. We observe that the gaps in KOL statements are not random deficiencies but a structured separation: KOLs express directional intent (what to buy or sell and why) while leaving execution decisions (when, how much, how long) systematically unspecified. Building on this observation, we propose an intent-preserving policy completion framework that treats KOL discourse as a partial trading policy and uses offline reinforcement learning to complete the missing execution decisions around the KOL-expressed intent. Experiments on multimodal KOL discourse from YouTube and X (2022-2025) show that KICL achieves the best return and Sharpe ratio on both platforms while maintaining zero unsupported entries and zero directional reversals, and ablations confirm that the full framework yields an 18.9% return improvement over the KOL-aligned baseline.

</details>


### [181] [Step-level Denoising-time Diffusion Alignment with Multiple Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14379)
*Qi Zhang,Dawei Wang,Shaofeng Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MSDDA：一种无需重新训练的多目标扩散模型对齐框架，通过步级RL公式化获得闭式解的最优去噪分布，在去噪时融合单目标基础模型，无近似误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类偏好本质上是多元的，扩散模型需要平衡多个下游目标（如美学质量和图文一致性）。现有多目标方法要么依赖昂贵的多目标RL微调，要么在去噪时融合单独对齐的模型，但通常需要奖励值（或其梯度）访问权限，和/或引入近似误差。

Method: 提出MSDDA（多目标步级去噪时扩散对齐）：1）重新审视扩散模型的RL微调问题，引入步级RL公式化解决最优策略识别难题；2）构建无需重新训练的框架，获得最优反向去噪分布的闭式解，均值和方差直接用单目标基础模型表示；3）证明该去噪时目标与步级RL微调完全等价，无近似误差。

Result: 数值结果表明，该方法优于现有的去噪时方法。理论证明MSDDA的去噪时目标与步级RL微调完全等价，不引入近似误差。

Conclusion: MSDDA提供了一种高效、精确的多目标扩散模型对齐框架，无需重新训练即可融合多个单目标基础模型，在去噪时实现最优策略，为实际应用中的多元人类偏好对齐问题提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a powerful tool for aligning diffusion models with human preferences, typically by optimizing a single reward function under a KL regularization constraint. In practice, however, human preferences are inherently pluralistic, and aligned models must balance multiple downstream objectives, such as aesthetic quality and text-image consistency. Existing multi-objective approaches either rely on costly multi-objective RL fine-tuning or on fusing separately aligned models at denoising time, but they generally require access to reward values (or their gradients) and/or introduce approximation error in the resulting denoising objectives. In this paper, we revisit the problem of RL fine-tuning for diffusion models and address the intractability of identifying the optimal policy by introducing a step-level RL formulation. Building on this, we further propose Multi-objective Step-level Denoising-time Diffusion Alignment (MSDDA), a retraining-free framework for aligning diffusion models with multiple objectives, obtaining the optimal reverse denoising distribution in closed form, with mean and variance expressed directly in terms of single-objective base models. We prove that this denoising-time objective is exactly equivalent to the step-level RL fine-tuning, introducing no approximation error. Moreover, we provide numerical results, which indicate our method outperforms existing denoising-time approaches.

</details>


### [182] [Auxiliary Finite-Difference Residual-Gradient Regularization for PINNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14472)
*Stavros Kassinos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种混合PINN设计，其中PDE残差保持自动微分基础，而有限差分仅出现在惩罚采样残差场梯度的弱辅助项中，用于正则化残差场而不替换PDE残差本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PINN通常使用单一标量损失函数，即使关注的具体物理量更为特定。研究旨在开发一种混合设计，通过有限差分正则化残差场梯度，提高特定物理量（如边界通量）的预测精度。

Method: 采用两阶段实验设计：第一阶段为受控泊松基准测试，比较基线PINN、FD残差梯度正则器和匹配的AD残差梯度基线；第二阶段将相同逻辑应用于三维环形热传导基准测试（PINN3D），在波浪状外壁附近实现体拟合壳层正则化。

Result: 在Kourkoutas-beta优化器下，固定壳层权重5e-4的配置最可靠：相对于无壳层项的匹配运行，外壁边界条件RMSE从1.22e-2降至9.29e-4，壁通量RMSE从9.21e-3降至9.63e-4。Adam优化器在初始学习率降至1e-3时也可用，但其壳层效益不如Kourkoutas-beta稳健。

Conclusion: 研究支持混合PINN的针对性观点：当辅助FD正则器与关注的物理量（如外壁通量）对齐时最为有效。该方法在保持自动微分PDE残差的同时，通过有限差分正则化残差场梯度，显著提高了特定物理量的预测精度。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) are often selected by a single scalar loss even when the quantity of interest is more specific. We study a hybrid design in which the governing PDE residual remains automatic-differentiation (AD) based, while finite differences (FD) appear only in a weak auxiliary term that penalizes gradients of the sampled residual field. The FD term regularizes the residual field without replacing the PDE residual itself.
  We examine this idea in two stages. Stage 1 is a controlled Poisson benchmark comparing a baseline PINN, the FD residual-gradient regularizer, and a matched AD residual-gradient baseline. Stage 2 transfers the same logic to a three-dimensional annular heat-conduction benchmark (PINN3D), where baseline errors concentrate near a wavy outer wall and the auxiliary grid is implemented as a body-fitted shell adjacent to the wall.
  In Stage 1, the FD regularizer reproduces the main effect of residual-gradient control while exposing a trade-off between field accuracy and residual cleanliness. In Stage 2, the shell regularizer improves the application-facing quantities, namely outer-wall flux and boundary-condition behavior. Across seeds 0-5 and 100k epochs, the most reliable tested configuration is a fixed shell weight of 5e-4 under the Kourkoutas-beta optimizer regime: relative to a matched run without the shell term, it reduces the mean outer-wall BC RMSE from 1.22e-2 to 9.29e-4 and the mean wall-flux RMSE from 9.21e-3 to 9.63e-4. Adam with beta2=0.999 becomes usable when the initial learning rate is reduced to 1e-3, although its shell benefit is less robust than under Kourkoutas-beta. Overall, the results support a targeted view of hybrid PINNs: an auxiliary-only FD regularizer is most valuable when it is aligned with the physical quantity of interest, here the outer-wall flux.

</details>


### [183] [Scouting By Reward: VLM-TO-IRL-Driven Player Selection For Esports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14474)
*Qing Yan,Wenyu Yang,Yufei Wang,Wenhao Ma,Linchong Hu,Yifei Jin,Anton Dahbura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将电竞选手评估重构为逆强化学习问题，通过从职业选手演示中学习奖励函数，基于风格匹配度对候选人进行排名


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电竞选秀工作流程依赖手动视频回顾和聚合性能指标，无法捕捉决定选手是否符合特定战术原型的细微决策模式，需要更精准的风格评估方法

Method: 提出新颖的选手选择框架，采用多模态双分支架构：一支编码来自高分辨率游戏遥测的结构化状态-动作轨迹，另一支编码从广播画面生成的时序对齐战术伪解说；通过生成对抗模仿学习目标融合表示，判别器学习精英选手的独特机械和战术特征

Result: 框架从通用技能评估转向基于奖励的选秀，提供可扩展的工作流程感知数字孪生系统，支持数据驱动的阵容构建和大规模候选人池的定向人才发现

Conclusion: 通过将基于风格的选手评估重构为逆强化学习问题，该框架能够学习职业选手特定的奖励函数，使组织能够根据候选人与目标明星选手的风格匹配度进行排名，实现更精准的选秀决策

Abstract: Traditional esports scouting workflows rely heavily on manual video review and aggregate performance metrics, which often fail to capture the nuanced decision-making patterns necessary to determine if a prospect fits a specific tactical archetype. To address this, we reframe style-based player evaluation in esports as an Inverse Reinforcement Learning (IRL) problem. In this paper, we introduce a novel player selection framework that learns professional-specific reward functions from logged gameplay demonstrations, allowing organizations to rank candidates by their stylistic alignment with a target star player. Our proposed architecture utilizes a multimodal, two-branch intake: one branch encodes structured state-action trajectories derived from high-resolution in-game telemetry, while the second encodes temporally aligned tactical pseudo-commentary generated by Vision-Language Models (VLMs) from broadcast footage. These representations are fused and evaluated via a Generative Adversarial Imitation Learning (GAIL) objective, where a discriminator learns to capture the unique mechanical and tactical signatures of elite professionals. By transitioning from generic skill estimation to scouting "by reward," this framework provides a scalable, workflow-aware digital twin system that enables data-driven roster construction and targeted talent discovery across massive candidate pools.

</details>


### [184] [CI-CBM: Class-Incremental Concept Bottleneck Model for Interpretable Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14519)
*Amirhosein Javadi,Tuomas Oikarinen,Tara Javidi,Tsui-Wei Weng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CI-CBM是一种用于类增量学习的可解释概念瓶颈模型，通过概念正则化和伪概念生成技术，在保持模型可解释性的同时有效缓解灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类增量学习中的灾难性遗忘问题严重，现有方法往往需要在模型可解释性和准确性之间做出妥协。需要一种既能保持可解释决策过程，又能维持高性能的类增量学习方法。

Method: 提出ClassIncremental Concept Bottleneck Model (CI-CBM)，采用概念正则化和伪概念生成技术。概念正则化帮助模型在学习新任务时保留先前学到的概念知识，伪概念生成则用于处理新任务中可能缺失的概念信息。

Result: 在7个数据集上的评估显示，CI-CBM性能与黑盒模型相当，且比之前的可解释方法平均提升36%的准确率。模型既能提供个体输入的局部解释，也能展示全局决策规则。

Conclusion: CI-CBM证明了在增量学习过程中可以保持人类可理解的概念知识而不牺牲模型性能，该方法在预训练和非预训练场景下都有效，为可解释的类增量学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting remains a fundamental challenge in continual learning, in which models often forget previous knowledge when fine-tuned on a new task. This issue is especially pronounced in class incremental learning (CIL), which is the most challenging setting in continual learning. Existing methods to address catastrophic forgetting often sacrifice either model interpretability or accuracy. To address this challenge, we introduce ClassIncremental Concept Bottleneck Model (CI-CBM), which leverage effective techniques, including concept regularization and pseudo-concept generation to maintain interpretable decision processes throughout incremental learning phases. Through extensive evaluation on seven datasets, CI-CBM achieves comparable performance to black-box models and outperforms previous interpretable approaches in CIL, with an average 36% accuracy gain. CICBM provides interpretable decisions on individual inputs and understandable global decision rules, as shown in our experiments, thereby demonstrating that human understandable concepts can be maintained during incremental learning without compromising model performance. Our approach is effective in both pretrained and non-pretrained scenarios; in the latter, the backbone is trained from scratch during the first learning phase. Code is publicly available at github.com/importAmir/CI-CBM.

</details>


### [185] [CSRA: Controlled Spectral Residual Augmentation for Robust Sepsis Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14532)
*Honglin Guo,Rihao Chang,He Jiao,Weizhi Nie,Zhongheng Zhang,Yuehao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CSRA：一种用于ICU短窗口多系统时间序列的受控谱残差增强框架，通过系统级表示提取和谱域残差扰动生成临床合理的轨迹变化，提升脓毒症风险预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脓毒症风险预测在ICU中具有重要临床意义，但短窗口预测面临挑战：短观察窗口提供有限历史证据，而长预测视野减少有效监督样本。需要解决数据增强在临床时间序列中的结构化和临床合理性问题。

Method: CSRA框架：1）按临床系统分组变量，提取系统级和全局表示；2）在谱域进行输入自适应残差扰动，生成结构化且临床合理的轨迹变化；3）端到端训练下游预测器，使用锚点一致性损失和控制器正则化提升增强稳定性和可控性。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV脓毒症队列实验中，CSRA在多个下游模型中表现一致竞争且通常更优：回归误差降低10.2%（MSE）和3.7%（MAE），分类任务也获得一致增益。在更短观察窗口、更长预测视野和更小训练数据规模下保持更优性能，在外部临床数据集（ZiGongICUinfection）上同样有效。

Conclusion: CSRA通过受控谱残差增强有效解决了ICU短窗口时间序列预测的数据增强问题，生成临床合理的轨迹变化，在临床约束设置下展现出更强的鲁棒性和泛化能力，为脓毒症风险预测提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of future risk and disease progression in sepsis is clinically important for early warning and timely intervention in intensive care. However, short-window sepsis prediction remains challenging, because shorter observation windows provide limited historical evidence, whereas longer prediction horizons reduce the number of patient trajectories with valid future supervision. To address this problem, we propose CSRA, a Controlled Spectral Residual Augmentation framework for short-window multi-system ICU time series. CSRA first groups variables by clinical systems and extracts system-level and global representations. It then performs input-adaptive residual perturbation in the spectral domain to generate structured and clinically plausible trajectory variations. To improve augmentation stability and controllability, CSRA is trained end-to-end with the downstream predictor under a unified objective, together with anchor consistency loss and controller regularization. Experiments on a MIMIC-IV sepsis cohort across multiple downstream models show that CSRA is consistently competitive and often superior, reducing regression error by 10.2\% in MSE and 3.7\% in MAE over the non-augmentation baseline, while also yielding consistent gains on classification. CSRA further maintains more favorable performance under shorter observation windows, longer prediction horizons, and smaller training data scales, while also remaining effective on an external clinical dataset~(ZiGongICUinfection), indicating stronger robustness and generalizability in clinically constrained settings.

</details>


### [186] [An unsupervised decision-support framework for multivariate biomarker analysis in athlete monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14534)
*Fernando Barcelos Rosito,Sebastião De Jesus Menezes,Simone Ferreira Sturza,Adriana Seixas,Muriel Figueredo Franco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无监督多变量框架，从运动员生物标志物数据中识别潜在生理状态，无需损伤标签，区分机械损伤与代谢应激，发现传统单变量监测遗漏的沉默风险表型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动员监测面临小样本、生物标志物尺度异质、重复采样可行性有限、缺乏可靠损伤真实标签等限制，这些因素降低了传统单变量和二元风险模型的解释性和实用性。

Method: 提出模块化计算框架，在联合生物标志物空间中操作，整合预处理、临床安全筛查、无监督聚类和基于质心的生理解释。仅使用业余足球运动员竞争微周期数据学习特征。通过合成数据增强评估鲁棒性和可扩展性。使用Ward层次聚类支持监测和病因学区分，高斯混合模型支持高维设置下的结构稳定性分析。

Result: 框架识别出能区分机械损伤与代谢应激同时保持稳态状态的连贯特征。合成数据增强证明了可行性，并检测到通常被单变量监测遗漏的潜在沉默风险表型。结构分析表明框架在增强和高维设置下具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架能够从多变量生物标志物数据中无需损伤标签即可解释性地识别潜在生理状态。通过区分机制并揭示传统监测未捕获的沉默风险模式，为个体化运动员监测和决策制定提供可操作的见解。

Abstract: Purpose. Athlete monitoring is constrained by small cohorts, heterogeneous biomarker scales, limited feasibility of repeated sampling, and the lack of reliable injury ground truth. These limitations reduce the interpretability and utility of traditional univariate and binary risk models. This study addresses these challenges by proposing an unsupervised multivariate framework to identify latent physiological states in athletes using real data. Methods. We propose a modular computational framework that operates in the joint biomarker space, integrating preprocessing, clinical safety screening, unsupervised clustering, and centroid-based physiological interpretation. Profiles are learned exclusively from amateur soccer players during a competitive microcycle. Synthetic data augmentation evaluates robustness and scalability. Ward hierarchical clustering supports monitoring and etiological differentiation, while Gaussian Mixture Models (GMM) enable structural stability analysis in high-dimensional settings. Results. The framework identifies coherent profiles that distinguish mechanical damage from metabolic stress while preserving homeostatic states. Synthetic data augmentation demonstrates feasibility and detection of latent silent risk phenotypes typically missed by univariate monitoring. Structural analyses indicate robustness under augmentation and higher-dimensional settings. Conclusion. The framework enables interpretable identification of latent physiological states from multivariate biomarker data without injury labels. By distinguishing mechanisms and revealing silent risk patterns not captured by conventional monitoring, it provides actionable insights for individualized athlete monitoring and decision making.

</details>


### [187] [Predicting Post-Traumatic Epilepsy from Clinical Records using Large Language Model Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14547)
*Wenhui Cui,Nicholas Swingle,Anand A. Joshi,Dileep Nair,Richard M. Leahy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 使用预训练大语言模型编码临床记录，结合梯度提升树分类器，实现创伤后癫痫的早期预测，AUC-ROC达0.892


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创伤后癫痫（PTE）是创伤性脑损伤后的严重并发症，早期预测面临临床数据异质性、阳性病例有限、依赖资源密集型神经影像数据等挑战。研究探索是否仅使用常规收集的急性期临床记录就能支持早期PTE预测。

Method: 使用TRACK-TBI队列的精选子集，开发自动化PTE预测框架：1）将预训练大语言模型作为固定特征提取器编码临床记录；2）评估表格特征、LLM生成嵌入和混合特征表示；3）在分层交叉验证下使用梯度提升树分类器进行评估。

Result: LLM嵌入通过捕捉上下文临床信息，相比仅使用表格特征实现了性能提升。最佳性能通过模态感知特征融合策略（结合表格特征和LLM嵌入）实现，AUC-ROC为0.892，AUPRC为0.798。急性创伤后癫痫发作、损伤严重程度、神经外科干预和ICU住院是关键预测因素。

Conclusion: 常规急性期临床记录包含适合早期PTE风险预测的信息，使用LLM嵌入结合梯度提升树分类器的方法具有临床实用性，可作为影像学预测的有前景补充。
